{D107}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya lang ka a ba tA ra ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa lang kar gshegs pa'i theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus 
[\[56a.2\]]gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das lang ka'i grong rdal na/_rgya mtsho'i ri ma la ya'i rtse mo rin po che'i rigs dang /_me tog sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa la dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sangs rgyas kyi zhing sna tshogs pa nas 'dus pa ting nge 
[\[56a.3\]]'dzin dang /_dbang dang stobs dang /_mngon par shes pa du mas rnam par rol pa/_byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen po la sogs pa dag sangs rgyas thams cad kyi phyag gi dbang bskur bas dbang bskur ba bdag gi sems snang ba'i spyod yul yongs su shes pa'i don la mkhas pa/_
[\[56a.4\]]sems can gyi sems sna tshogs dang /_gzugs dang tshul dang /_'dul ba rnam pa tha dad pa'i cha byad 'chang ba/_chos lnga dang /_rang bzhin dang /_rnam par shes pa dang /_bdag med pa gnyis khong du chud par rtogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dag gi tshogs chen po dang thabs 
[\[56a.5\]]cig tu bzhugs te/_yang de'i tshe na bcom ldan 'das rgya mtsho'i klu'i rgyal po'i gnas nas zhag bdun lon te byung nas brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_klu'i bu mo bye ba du mas bsu ba dang lang ka'i ri ma la ya la gzigs te/_lang ka'i grong rdal gyi ri ma la ya'i rtse mo 'dir sngon gyi de 
[\[56a.6\]]bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i ye shes/_rtog ge'i lta ba mu stegs can dang /_nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang /_'phags pa'i yul ma yin pa de dag gis bsgoms pa'i chos bstan 
[\[56a.7\]]te/_ngas kyang de nyid du gnod sbyin gyi bdag po 'bod 'grogs kyi phyir de nyid bsnyad cing chos bstan to snyam nas 'dzum pa mdzad do/_/de nas gnod sbyin gyi bdag po 'bod 'grogs de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis bcom ldan 'das rgya mtsho'i klu'i rgyal po'i gnas nas 
[\[56b\]]
[\[56b.1\]]byung ste/_brgya byin dang tshangs pa dang /_klu'i bu mo brgya stong du mas bskor cing mdun du bdar nas rgya mtsho'i rlabs la gzigs te/_kun gzhi rnam par shes pa'i rgya mtsho la 'jug pa'i rnam par shes pa yul gyi rlung gis bskyod cing de dag gi sems 'byung bar gzigs te/_ched du 
[\[56b.2\]]brjod pa ched du brjod pa mdzad do/_/zhes thos nas/_bdag song la bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba btab ste lang kar gshegs par bya'o/_/de ni bdag dang lha dang mi rnams la yun ring po'i don dang /_phan pa dang bde bar 'gyur ro snyam ste/_de nas gnod sbyin gyi bdag po 'bod 
[\[56b.3\]]'grogs 'khor dang bcas pa me tog gi gzhal med khang du zhugs nas/_bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin pa dang /_'khor dang bcas te gzhal med khang nas babs nas rnga dang sil snyan rnams rab tu bsgrags te/_bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas pi bang gi 
[\[56b.4\]]khog pa in dra nI la la bai dUr+ya dang /_mu sa ras spras pa/_gos ngur ka pri yang ku 'dra ba rin thang med pas tshar du dpyangs te/_'khor rnams rab tu dga' bar byas nas/_glu'i dbyangs ShaD+dzA dang /_ri Shab+ha dang /_gAn d+ha ra dang /_d+hai bA ta dang /_ni ShA da dang /_ma d+h+ya ma dang /_kau shi 
[\[56b.5\]]ka dang /_glu'i dbyangs mang por sbyar ba la sogs pa dang ldan zhing /_rgyud rjes su 'thun pa dang pi bang gi nang du chud par byas te tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyangs kyis glu blangs pa/_sems kyi rang bzhin tshul nyid chos kyi gter/_/bdag med lta dang rnam bral dri ma med/_/so so rang rig 
[\[56b.6\]]shes pa ston pa yi/_/chos tshul mgon po 'dir yang bshad du gsol/_/dge ba'i chos kyis yang dag bsags pa'i sku/_/bde gshegs 'phrul pas sprul pa ston pa ste/_/so so rang rig rtogs pa'i chos dgyes pa/_/thub pa deng ni lang kar gshegs pa'i dus/_/lang ka 
[\[56b.7\]]'di na sngon gyi rgyal ba dang /_/de yi sras rnams gzugs mang 'chang ba bzhugs/_/mgon po 'dir ni chos rab bshad du gsol/_/gzugs mang ldan pa'i gnod sbyin kun kyang nyan/_/de ltar lang ka'i bdag po 'bod 'grogs kyis to Ta ka'i rgyud du glu blangs nas yang tshigs su bcad 
[\[57a\]]
[\[57a.1\]]pa'i dbyangs kyis glu blangs pa/_/bcom ldan 'das ni dgung bdun te/_/chu srin gnas kyi rgya mtsho yi/_/rgya mtsho'i dbang po'i gnas nas ni/_/slad du byung ste 'gram na bzhugs/_/sangs rgyas bzhugs ma thag tu yang /_/ne tso sa ra nas brgyan pa'i/_/
[\[57a.2\]]gnod sbyin rnam pa mang po dang /_/'bod 'grogs lha mo lhan cig tu/_/mgon po gang na bzhugs pa yi/_/lam der 'phrul gyis song nas ni/_/me tog bzhon las babs nas kyang /_/de bzhin gshegs la mchod phyag 'tshal/_/rgyal ba'i dbang pos byin brlabs nas/_/
[\[57a.3\]]de la mngon par mi bsnyad pa/_/bdag ni 'bod 'grogs mgrin bcu ste/_/srin po'i dbang po 'dir mchis so/_/grong rdal 'di na su gnas dang /_/bdag gi lang ka rjes su zung /_/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas snga mas kyang /_/so so rang rig spyod yul ni/_/rtse mo rin chen brgyan 
[\[57a.4\]]pa yi/_/grong rdal dbus su rab tu bshad/_/bcom ldan gyis kyang de nyid kyi/_/rtse mo rin chen brgyan pa la/_/rgyal ba'i sras kyis yongs bskor cing /_/rdul med chos ni bshad du gsol/_/gang rnams lang kar gnas pa dang /_/bdag cag de ring nyan par 'tshal/_/
[\[57a.5\]]bshad pa'i tshul las rnam grol ba/_/so so rang rig spyod yul te/_/lang kar gshegs pa'i mdo sde ni/_/sangs rgyas snga mas gsungs pa'o/_/rgyal ba'i sras kyis yongs bskor cing /_/sngon gyi sangs rgyas dag gis kyang /_/mdo sde 'di ni bshad par dran/_/
[\[57a.6\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad du gsol/_/ma 'ongs dus na 'byung ba yi/_/sangs rgyas sangs rgyas sras dag kyang /_/'phrul gyi tshul te 'di nyid ni/_/rtse mo rin chen brgyan pa la/_/gnod sbyin rnams la thugs brtse phyir/_/'dren pa rnams ni ston par 'gyur/_/'phrul 
[\[57a.7\]]gyi lang ka'i grong rdal 'di/_/mdangs dga' rin chen sna tshogs dang /_/thel sdings rdo stengs dga' ba dang /_/rin chen dra ba'i bla res brgyan/_/rnal 'byor nyes dang rnam bral ba/_/so so rang rig sems pa yi/_/bcom ldan 'di na gnod sbyin dag_/sangs rgyas snga mas 
[\[57b\]]
[\[57b.1\]]mdzad srung ba/_/theg chen tshul la dad pa yi/_/mngon par zhen cing gzhan 'dzud pa/_/gnod sbyin mo dang gnod sbyin bu/_/theg chen rtogs par 'tshal ba mchis/_/lang ka ma la ya yi rir/_/ston pa sangs rgyas gshegs su gsol/_/bum pa rna la sogs pa 
[\[57b.2\]]rnams/_/srin po grong rdal gnas pa dag_/theg pa che la rton pa rnams/_/so so rang rig nyan par 'tshal/_/sangs rgyas rnams la bgyi ba bgyis/_/da ltar na yang shin tu bgyid/_/bdag la rjes su thugs brtse'i phyir/_/lang kar sras dang bcas te gshegs/_/
[\[57b.3\]]khyim dang lha mo tshogs rnams dang /_/se mo do yang sna tshogs dang /_/mdangs dga' a sho ka yi tshal/_/thub pa chen po bzhes su gsol/_/bdag ni rgyal sras de dag dang /_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi bka' bzhin bgyid/_/bdag gis mi dbul gcig ma mchis/_/
[\[57b.4\]]thub pa chen po thugs brtser dgongs/_/de yi tshig de gsan pa dang /_/srid gsum dbang pos gsungs pa ni/_/gnod sbyin dbang po 'das pa yi/_/mgon pos rin chen ri bo la/_/so so rang rig chos rnams bstan/_/khyod nyid la yang thugs brtser dgongs/_/rin chen 
[\[57b.5\]]rnam brgyan ri bo la/_/ma 'ongs rnams kyang gsung bar 'gyur/_/'di ni rnal 'byor ldan pa dag_/mthong ba'i chos la spyod pa'i gnas/_/nga dang sras rnams dag gis kyang /_/gnod sbyin dbang po brtser dgongs rigs/_/bcom ldan gnang nas mi gsung bar/_/zhi ba'i 
[\[57b.6\]]blo yis rnam par bzhugs/_/'bod 'grogs kyis ni phul ba yi/_/me tog bzhon par bzhugs par gyur/_/de nyid du ni 'bod 'grogs dang /_/'jigs med rgyal sras gzhan yang ste/_/lha mos bzhad gad rol mo yis/_/rab tu mchod cing grong du gshegs/_/dga' 
[\[57b.7\]]ba'i grong rdal der gshegs nas/_/mchod pa rab tu yang brnyes so/_/'bod 'grogs la sogs gnod sbyin gyi/_/sde dang gnod sbyin mos kyang mchod/_/gnod sbyin bu mo gnod sbyin bus/_/rin po che yi dra bas mchod/_/'bod 'grogs kyis kyang sangs rgyas kyi/_/rin 
[\[58a\]]
[\[58a.1\]]chen brgyan pa'i se mo do/_/rgyal ba'i sras dang rgyal ba yi/_/stod kyi yan lag rnams la btags/_/bcom ldan rgyal sras mkhas bcas pa/_/mchod pa phul ba bzhes nas ni/_/so so rang rig spyod yul gyi/_/chos ni rnam par bstan pa 
[\[58a.2\]]mdzad/_/gnod sbyin sde dang 'bod 'grogs kyis/_/smra ba rnams kyi mchog mchod de/_/blo gros chen po rab mchod cing /_/gsol ba yang dang yang 'debs pa/_/khyod ni sangs rgyas thams cad la/_/rang rig spyod yul 'dri ba yin/_/'di ni bdag dang gnod sbyin 
[\[58a.3\]]dang /_/rgyal ba'i sras rnams nyan pa mchis/_/khyod la rgyal sras mkhas pa dang /_/gnod sbyin rnams kyang gsol ba 'debs/_/smra ba'i nang na smra chen khyod/_/rnal 'byor can gyi'ang rnal 'byor zhugs/_/khyod la gus par gsol 'debs kyis/_/'jigs pa 
[\[58a.4\]]med pas tshul dris shig_/rang rgyal nyan thos rnams dang ni/_/mu stegs nyes las rnam par grol/_/so so rang rig chos nyid gtsang /_/sangs rgyas sa ni thob byed pa'o/_/rtse mo rin chen brgyan pa dang /_/gzhan yang lha rdzas rnams kyi ni/_/rin chen bye bas brgyan 
[\[58a.5\]]pa dag_/bcom ldan 'das kyis der sprul te/_/mgon po ri bo re re la/_/nyid kyi sku ni rab tu bstan/_/'bod 'grogs gnod sbyin de dag na/_/re re la ni rnam par gnas/_/thams cad 'khor ni ma bri bar/_/re re na yang shin tu snang /_/de na zhing rnams thams 
[\[58a.6\]]cad dang /_/gang yang de na 'dren pa dang /_/de na srin po'i dbang po rnams/_/gang dag lang kar gnas pa'ang snang /_/de dang 'gran pa'i lang ka dang /_/a sho ka yi tshal gzhan dang /_/nags tshal bzang po de dag kyang /_/rgyal ba yis ni mngon par sprul/_/mgon 
[\[58a.7\]]po ri rnams re re la/_/blo gros chen pos bskul ba dang /_/so so rang rig rjod pa yis/_/chos ni gnod sbyin rnams la bstan/_/stong phrag brgya pa'i mdo sde dag_/ri bo la ni ma lus bshad/_/ston pa dang ni rgyal sras rnams/_/de nyid du yang mi snang gyur/_/gnod sbyin 
[\[58b\]]
[\[58b.1\]]'bod 'grogs bdag gis lus/_/khyim na gnas par mthong ba dang /_/bsams pa 'di go ji zhig snyam/_/gang gis bshad de su yis thos/_/ci zhig mthong gi gang gis mthong /_/grong khyer de dag gang du song /_/sangs rgyas de dag zhing de rnams/_/rin chen 
[\[58b.2\]]nags bzang de ga re/_/'di na sgyu ma'am rmi lam rol/_/dri za'i grong zhes bsgrags pa'am/_/rab rib yin nam smig rgyu'am/_/rmi lam mo sham bu byung ngam/_/bdag gis 'dir ni gang mthong ba/_/mgal me bskor tam du ba'am/_/'on te sems kyi spyod yul 
[\[58b.3\]]la/_/chos rnams kyi ni chos nyid dam/_/lta ba med cing blta ba'ang med/_/smra med smra bar bya med na/_/rang gi rtog pas myos pa yi/_/byis pa rnams kyis mi shes so/_/gzhan du rnam par rtog pa 'di/_/sangs rgyas dang ni chos ltar gnas/_/ji ltar mthong 
[\[58b.4\]]bzhin su lta ba/_/de dag 'dren pa mi mthong ngo /_/rnam par rtog pa mi 'jug cing /_/gang tshe sangs rgyas mi mthong la/_/'jug med sangs rgyas yin par ni/_/gal te mthong na de sangs rgyas/_/lang ka'i bdag po gnas shin tu gyur pa dang /_rang gi sems snang ba 
[\[58b.5\]]tsam du khong du chud pa rab tu rtogs ma thag tu rnam par rtog pa'i rgyu ba la gnas pa/_sngon gyi dge ba'i rtsa bas bskul ba/_gtsug lag thams cad la mkhas pa'i blo de bzhin nyid ji lta ba bzhin du mthong ba/_gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog pa/_bdag gi blo rnam par dpyod pa la mkhas 
[\[58b.6\]]pa/_rtog ge ba'i lta ba dang bral bar lta ba/_gzhan gyi dring la mi 'jog pa/_rnal 'byor chen po'i rnal 'byor can/_gzugs sna tshogs 'chang ba chen po thabs la mkhas pa khong du chud par rtogs pa/_gong nas gong du sa thams cad kyi rang gi mtshan nyid rtogs par bya ba la 
[\[58b.7\]]mkhas pa/_sems dang yid dang yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin dben pa la dga' ba/_rgyud gsum chad par lta ba/_rgyu dang mu stegs can thams cad kyi rgyu dang bral ba'i blor gyur pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po/_sangs rgyas kyi sa la nang du snyoms par zhugs pa 
[\[59a\]]
[\[59a.1\]]la gnas zhing sangs rgyas kyi blos nam mkha' dang nang nas sgra thos pa lang ka'i bdag po legs so legs so/_/yang lang ka'i bdag po khyod ni legs so/_/ji ltar khyod slob pa de ltar rnal 'byor can rnams kyis bslab par bya'o/_/ji ltar khyod kyis 
[\[59a.2\]]mthong ba de bzhin nyid du de bzhin gshegs pa rnams dang /_chos rnams la blta bar bya'o/_/rnam pa gzhan du bltas na chad pa la gnas par 'gyur ro/_/khyod kyi sems dang yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang bral zhing nang du spyod pas/_chos thams cad rnam par 
[\[59a.3\]]bsgom par bya ste/_phyi rol gyi don du lta ba la mngon par chags pas ni ma yin no/_/khyod kyis nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs can gyi mngon par rtogs pa dang /_tshig gi don gyi spyod yul du lhung ba'i lta ba dang /_ting nge 'dzin du ma yin/_gtam 
[\[59a.4\]]dang sgrung la dga' bar ma yin/_rig byed kyi rang bzhin du lta bar ma yin/_rgyal po'i dbang gis dregs pa la lhung bar ma yin/_bsam gtan drug la sogs pa la bsam gtan bar ma yin te/_lang ka'i bdag po 'di ni rgyal ba'i sras rnal 'byor chen po'i rnal 'byor dang 
[\[59a.5\]]ldan pa/_pha rol rgol ba rab tu 'joms pa/_lta ba ngan pa sel ba/_bdag tu lta ba rnam par bzlog pa la mkhas pa/_blo zhib cing rnam par shes pa/_shin tu 'gyur ba la mkhas pa/_theg pa chen po la spyod pa rnams kyi dam tshig go/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bdag 
[\[59a.6\]]gi so so rang gi sa la 'jug pa khong du chud par bya ba'i phyir khyod kyis brtson par gyis shig_/de ltar byas na lang ka'i bdag po gang khyod kyis ting nge 'dzin la mkhas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pas yongs su bzung ba'i lam 'di ni phyir zhing gong nas gong du rnam par spyod pa yin te/_
[\[59a.7\]]ji ltar byis pa mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor la rnal 'byor can bdag tu 'dzin par snang ba'i mtshan nyid la mngon par chags pa 'byung ba dang /_yon tan dang rdzas kyi rjes su spyod pa/_ma rig pa'i rkyen du lta ba la mngon par chags pas mngon par zhen pa/_stong pa dang /_skye 
[\[59b\]]
[\[59b.1\]]bas g.yengs pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa la mngon par chags pa/_mtshan nyid du lhung ba'i bsam pas brtags pa/_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs can rjes su 'jug pa'i sgo'i spyod yul ni ma yin no/_/lang ka'i bdag po bdag gi so so rang gis rig pa 
[\[59b.2\]]rtogs par byed pa'i theg pa chen po khong du chud par byed pa 'di ni gzugs sna tshogs rab tu 'thob par byed pa'o/_/bye brag gi srid par skye ba yongs su 'thob par yang 'gyur ro/_/lang ka'i bdag po theg pa chen po'i rnal 'byor la 'jug pa 'di ni khebs pa'i g.yogs ma rnam 
[\[59b.3\]]par shes pa sna tshogs kyi rlabs zlog par byed pa yin te/_mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor gyi gnas su lhung ba ni ma yin no/_/lang ka'i bdag po mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor ni mu stegs can rnams kyi bdag tu mngon par zhen pa las byung ba'o/_/rnam par shes pa'i rang 
[\[59b.4\]]bzhin gnyis kyi don la chags par lta bas mu stegs can rnams kyi 'di yang rnal 'byor ma yin no/_/lang ka'i bdag po de legs so/_/de bzhin gshegs pa mthong zhing ji ltar rjes su bsams pa'i don de nyid rjes su soms shig dang de nyid ni de bzhin gshegs pa mthong 
[\[59b.5\]]ba yin no/_/de nas de'i skabs su 'bod 'grogs 'di snyam du sems te/_yang 'di ltar bdag gis bcom ldan 'das rnal 'byor thams cad la mnga' mdzad pa/_mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor rnam par zlog pa/_so so rang gis rig pa'i spyod yul ston pa/_'phrul pas 
[\[59b.6\]]sprul pa dang bral ba/_thugs su chud pa'i blo la gang gi phyir rnal 'byor can rnams rnal 'byor mngon par rtogs pa'i dus na ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa rnams khong du chud par 'gyur te/_de khong du chud pa'i phyir 'dis khong du chud do zhes rnal 'byor can rnams kyi rnal 
[\[59b.7\]]'byor gyi sgrar gdags te/_de'i phyir bdag gis thugs rje can nyon mongs pa 'bar ba'i rgyu dang /_rnam par rtog pa zad par byed pa/_rgyal ba'i sras rnams kyis yongs su bskor ba/_sems can thams cad kyi sems dang bsam pa'i rjes su zhugs pa/_thams cad du khyab 
[\[60a\]]
[\[60a.1\]]pa/_thams cad mkhyen pa la byed pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par log pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyis de ltar blta'o/_/de mthong bas ni khong du ma chud pa khong du chud par bya'o/_/rnam par mi rtog pa la spyod pa bde ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_snyoms par 'jug pas gnas pa de 
[\[60a.2\]]bzhin gshegs pas thugs su chud pa'i sa thob par byed pa bdag gis khong du chud par yang rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur ro snyam mo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe lang ka'i bdag po ma skyes pa'i chos la bzod pa thob par thugs su chud nas mgrin bcu la thugs brtse ba'i 
[\[60a.3\]]phyir rin po che mang pos brgyan pa/_rin po che'i dra bas bres pa'i bdag gi ri'i rtse mo la mdzes pa de lta bu nyid du bdag nyid bstan nas/_yang lang ka'i bdag po mgrin bcus mthong ba dang /_myong ba'i mdzes pa dang /_rtse mo la de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag 
[\[60a.4\]]par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mtshan rab sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis brgyan pa'i sku dang /_ri bo re re la bdag gi lus kyang de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi spyan snga na 'dug pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa blo gros chen po dang thabs 
[\[60a.5\]]cig so so rang gis rig pa'i spyod yul gyi gtam mdzad pa dang /_gnod sbyin gyis bskor cing bshad pa brjod pa'i gtam gsung ba dang /_mgon po dang bcas pa'i zhing de dag kyang mthong bar gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi sha'i spyan gyis 'khor la 
[\[60a.6\]]gzigs nas/_seng ge'i rgyal po ltar rnam par bsgyings te/_bzhad mo cher bzhad nas/_mdzod spu dang /_rtsib logs dang /_brla dang /_sked pa'i phyogs dang /_dpal gyi be'u dang /_spu'i khung bu thams cad nas ni 'od zer rab tu 'byin/_dus kyi mtha'i me bzhin 
[\[60a.7\]]du ni 'bar/_gzi brjid ni 'ja' lta bu/_'od kyi dkyil 'khor ni nyi ma shar ba dang 'dra bar shin tu 'bar/_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong ba rnams kyis ni nam mkha'i dbyings nas lta zhing ri rab kyi rtse mo la 'gran pa'i zom la bzhugs nas bzhad mo cher bzhad 
[\[60b\]]
[\[60b.1\]]do/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor de dang /_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa la sogs pa de dag 'di snyam du sems te/_rgyu gang dang rkyen gang gis de ltar bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad la mnga' mdzad pa sngon du 'dzum pas bzhad mo cher bzhad cing rang gi sku las 'od zer rab 
[\[60b.2\]]tu 'byin/_phyung nas kyang bdag gi so so rang gis rig pa'i 'phags pa'i ye shes mthong ba'i spyod yul ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba la dgongs pa rab tu bzhag ste/_gzigs mo med par seng ge'i lta bas phyogs su gzigs nas 'bod 'grogs kyi rnal 'byor rtogs pa rgyu ba la rjes su dgongs 
[\[60b.3\]]shing cang mi gsung bar gyur snyam mo/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po sngar gsol ba btab pas 'bod 'grogs la snying brtse ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor de dag gi sems dang /_bsam pa dang /_rnam par spyod pa yang shes shing ma 'ongs 
[\[60b.4\]]pa'i skye bo'i tshogs la'ang bltas nas bshad pa brjod pa la mngon par dga' ba'i sems can rnams dang /_sgra ji bzhin du mngon par zhen pa rnams dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor gyi stobs la mngon par zhen pa thams cad 'di ltar bcom

[\[65a.4\]]rnams su yis rab tu byung /_/sems can dngos po'i sna grangs dang /_/mngon par brjod pa su yis bskyed/_/rig pa'i gnas dang bzo yi rnams/_/gang gis ji ltar rab tu bshad/_/tshigs su bcad pa rnam pa du/_/tshigs bcad rkyang pa ji lta bu/_/rigs pa rnam pa du zhig mchis/_/
[\[65a.5\]]bshad pa dag kyang ji ltar 'gyur/_/skom dang kha zas sna tshogs dang /_/ji ltar 'khrig pa 'byung bar 'gyur/_/rgyal po 'khor los sgyur ba dang /_/khams kyi bdag po ji ltar lags/_/ji ltar rgyal srid srung bar 'gyur/_/lha yi rigs la rnam pa du/_/sa dang skar tshogs ci las 
[\[65a.6\]]gyur/_/nyi zla gnyis kyang ji lta bu/_/thar pa rnam pa du zhig mchis/_/rnal 'byor ldan pa rnam pa du/_/slob ma rnam pa du zhig mchis/_/slob dpon du yang ji ltar 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas rnam pa du zhig lags/_/skyes pa'i rabs la rnam pa du/_/bdud la rnam pa du zhig 
[\[65a.7\]]mchis/_/ya mtshan can yang rnam pa du/_/khyod kyi rang bzhin rnam pa du/_/ri mo rnam pa du zhig mchis/_/btags pa tsam yang ji lta bu/_/smra ba'i mchog gis bdag la gsungs/_/nam mkha'i sprin rlung ci las gyur/_/dran dang yid gzhungs ji ltar 'byung /_/shing gi 'khri 
[\[65b\]]
[\[65b.1\]]shing ji ltar mchis/_/srid gsum dbang po bdag la gsungs/_/glang po [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rta blun rnams/_/gzung du rung ba ci slad du/_/ngan pa'i mi rnams ci las gyur/_/bdag la sems lung sgyur bas gsungs/_/khams drug 'dzin pa ci slad du/_/'dod chen bar yang ji ltar

[\[66a\]]
[\[66a.1\]]du/_/lta ba dag kyang rnam pa du/_/dge slong dngos dang 'dul ba yang /_/ci slad ji ltar bdag la gsungs/_/rang rgyal rgyal sras nyan thos kyi/_/ci slad 'gyur ba rgyal po dang /_/snang ba med pa rgyal po yang /_/ci yi slad pa bdag la gsungs/_/'jig rten mngon

[\[66a.3\]]la gsungs/_/'od srung log par dad sel dang /_/gser thub kyang ni nga yin zhes/_/rgyal ba'i sras la gsung ba de/_/thub pa chen po ci slad gsungs/_/bdag dang sems can cis med brjod/_/rtag dang 'jig gtam ji lta bur/_/ci slad sems tsam yang dag nyid/_/thams

[\[73b.1\]]po'i rnam par ni/_/mi yi rnam par shes pa gda'/_/dba' rlabs sems dang 'thun pa'i chos/_/ci slad thub chen bshad du gsol/_/sngon po dmar po'i rnam pa rnams/_/dba' rlabs dag la ma mchis te/_/mtshan nyid don du byis pa la/_/sems kyi 'jug pa bshad 
[\[73b.2\]]pa'o/_/de la 'jug pa med pa ste/_/rang gi sems ni gzung ba spangs/_/gzung ba yod na 'dzin pa yang /_/dba' rlabs 'thun par sgrub par 'gyur/_/lus dang longs spyod gnas lta bur/_/mi yi rnam par shes par snang /_/de phyir de yi 'jug pa ni/_/dba' 
[\[73b.3\]]rlabs dag dang 'dra bar snang /_/dba' rlabs dngos por rgya mtsho ni/_/gar byed rnam par rig 'gyur na/_/kun gzhi 'jug pa de bzhin du/_/ci yi phyir na blos mi rig_/byis pa blo dang mi ldan pas/_/kun gzhi rgya mtsho ji bzhin du/_/dba' rlabs 'byung ba'i chos 'thun phyir/_/
[\[73b.4\]]dpe yis nye bar sbyar ba yin/_/skye bo bzang ngan bar ma la/_/gdugs 'char 'gyur ba ji bzhin du/_/de bzhin 'jig rten sgron ma khyod/_/bus pa rnams la yang dag ston/_/chos la rab tu bzhugs mdzad cing /_/ci slad de bzhin nyid mi gsung /_/de ste de bzhin nyid gsung 
[\[73b.5\]]na/_/sems la de bzhin nyid ma mchis/_/ji ltar rgya mtsho'i dba' rlabs dang /_/me long rmi lam ji bzhin du/_/cig cha'i dus su snang ba bzhin/_/sems kyang rang gi spyod yul la/_/yul dang rnam par mi ldan pas/_/rim gyis 'jug par rab tu 'byung /_/rnam par shes 
[\[73b.6\]]pas rnam par rig_/yid kyis de bzhin snyems par byed/_/lnga po rnams la snang bar mngon/_/mnyam par bzhag la rim pa med/_/ri mo'i mkhan po la la 'am/_/ri mo mkhan gyi slob ma'ang rung /_/ri mo'i don du tshon 'gyed pa/_/bshad pa 'di yang de dang 'dra/_/tshon 
[\[73b.7\]]la ri mo yod pa min/_/pir dang snod la'ang de bzhin med/_/sems can drang ba'i don gyi phyir/_/tshon gyis ri mo rnam par brtag_/yang dag la ni yi ge med/_/bshad pa dag ni 'khrul pa'o/_/chos rnams la ni gnas bcas shing /_/rnal 'byor can la yang dag bstan/_/
[\[74a\]]
[\[74a.1\]]so so rang rig yang dag ni/_/brtag dang rtog pas yang dag spangs/_/rgyal ba'i sras la 'di bstan gyi/_/byis pa rnams la bshad ma yin/_/ji ltar sgyu ma sna tshogs dag_/snang yang yod pa ma yin te/_/de bzhin bshad pa sna tshogs kyang /_/bshad 
[\[74a.2\]]pa rnam par 'khrul pa yin/_/gzhan la gang zhig bstan pa de/_/gzhan la bshad par mi rung ste/_/ji ltar nad pa so so la/_/sman pa sman rnams gtong ba ltar/_/sangs rgyas de bzhin sems can la/_/sems tsam du yang rab tu gsung /_/mgon po rnams ni gang 
[\[74a.3\]]gsung ba'i/_/so so rang rig spyod yul ni/_/rtog pa can gyi yul ma yin/_/nyan thos rnams kyi'ang de bzhin min/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' rang gi sems snang ba'i gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa la rnam par rtog pa'i spyod yul yongs su shes par 
[\[74a.4\]]'dod pas 'du ba dang /_'du 'dzi dang /_gnyid dang /_sgrib pa med par bya'o/_/srod dang tho rangs dang /_nam gyi gung la mi nyal ba'i sbyor ba la brtson par bya'o/_/mu stegs can dang /_ngan pa'i gtsug lag dang /_gtam dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi 
[\[74a.5\]]theg pa'i mtshan nyid dang bral bar bya'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos rang gi sems snang ba la rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid khong du chud par rtogs par bya'o/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos sems dang shes rab dang /_
[\[74a.6\]]ye shes kyi mtshan nyid rnam par dgod pa la gnas te/_gong du 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid gsum la brtson par bya'o/_/de la blo gros chen po gong du 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid gsum po gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_snang ba med pa'i mtshan nyid dang /_sangs rgyas 
[\[74a.7\]]thams cad kyi rang gi smon lam gyi byin gyis rlabs kyi mtshan nyid dang /_'phags pa so so rang gi ye shes kyi mtshan nyid de/_de dag khong du chud pas rnal 'byor can bong bu theng po lta bur sems dang /_shes rab dang /_ye shes kyi mtshan nyid spangs te/_rgyal ba'i sras kyi sa brgyad po 
[\[74b\]]
[\[74b.1\]]thob nas de'i gong du mtshan nyid gsum la brtson par byed do/_/de la blo gros chen po thams cad snang ba med pa'i mtshan nyid ni nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs can gyi mtshan nyid yongs su 'dris pa las 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po byin gyis rlabs kyi mtshan nyid ni 
[\[74b.2\]]sngon gyi sangs rgyas rnams kyi rang gi smon lam gyi byin gyis rlabs las 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po so so rang gis rig pa 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid ni sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang /_mtshan nyid thams cad la mngon par zhen pa med pa dang /_sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin 
[\[74b.3\]]gyi lus rab tu thob pas sangs rgyas kyi sa khong du chud pa'i rtogs pa rgyu ba las 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po 'di ni 'phags pa rnams kyi mtshan nyid gsum ste/_'phags pa'i mtshan nyid gsum dang ldan na 'phags pa rnams 'phags pa bdag gi so so rang gi ye shes kyi spyod yul khong du 
[\[74b.4\]]chud par 'gyur ro/_/de bas na blo gros chen po 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid gsum po la brtson par bya'o/_/de nas sangs rgyas thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor de dag gi sems 
[\[74b.5\]]dang /_bsam pa'i spyod pa rab tu shes nas 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi dngos po rab tu 'byed pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs bcom ldan 'das la zhus so/_/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams gang la gnas shing /_byang 
[\[74b.6\]]chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams la rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid du gtogs pa yongs su brtags pa'i rang bzhin rtogs pa rab tu phye ba'i tshul ston pa dang /_yongs su brtags pa'i rang bzhin rtogs pa rab tu phye ba'i tshul bye brag shin tu rtogs pa gang gis gang zag dang /_chos 
[\[74b.7\]]la bdag med pa'i rab tu rgyu ba shin tu sbyangs te/_sa rnams la mkhas par byas pa rnams nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs byed pa thams cad kyi bsam gtan dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa'i bde ba las 'das te/_de bzhin gshegs pas 
[\[75a\]]
[\[75a.1\]]mkhyen pa'i yul la 'jug pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i chos lnga dang /_rang bzhin rtogs pa las rnam par log pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi sku shes rab dang /_ye shes shin tu 'brel ba'i chos can sgyu ma'i spyod yul gyis mngon par grub pa/_sangs rgyas 
[\[75a.2\]]kyi zhing thams cad dang /_dga' ldan gyi pho brang dang /_'og min gyi gnas su nye bar bzhud pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rab tu thob par 'gyur ba/_'phags pa'i ye shes kyi dngos po rab tu 'byed pa'i rang bzhin chos kyi rnam grangs tshig brgya rtsa brgyad shin tu phye ba'i gnas bdag 
[\[75a.3\]]la bshad du gsol/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po 'di la mu stegs kyi lta ba can la la med pa la mngon par zhen pa dag_/rnam par rtog pa'i blo'i rgyu zad pa'i rang bzhin med pa'i phyir ri bong la rwa med do snyam ste/_ji ltar ri bong la ri bong gi 
[\[75a.4\]]rwa med pa de bzhin du chos thams cad la'ang rnam par rtog go/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang 'byung ba dang /_yon tan dang /_rdul dang rdzas dang dbyibs dang /_dpe tshul gyi khyad par mthong nas ri bong gi rwa med par mngon par chags shing zhen pa rnams ba lang gi rwa yod do/_/snyam du 
[\[75a.5\]]rnam par rtog go/_/blo gros chen po mtha' gnyis kyi lta bar lhung zhing sems tsam du ma rtogs pa'i blo can de dag de rang gi sems kyi khams rnam par rtog pas lus dang /_longs spyod dang /_gnas dang 'gro ba dang /_rnam par rtog pa tsam la blo gros chen po ri bong gi rwa yod pa dang /_
[\[75a.6\]]med pa las rnam par log pa brtag par mi bya'o/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po dngos po thams cad kyang yod pa dang med pa las rnam par log pa ste/_brtag par mi bya'o/_/blo gros chen po gang yang yod pa dang med pa las rnam par log pa'i ri bong gi rwa la med do snyam du rtog ste/_de dag 
[\[75a.7\]]gis phan tshun du ltos pa'i rgyu'i phyir ri bong gi rwa med do snyam du brtag par mi bya'o/_/blo gros chen po rdul phra rab kyi bar du brtags na dngos po mi dmigs te/_med pa'i phyir 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi spyod yul las rnam par log pa'i ba lang gi rwa yod par mi brtag go/_/de nas bcom ldan 
[\[75b\]]
[\[75b.1\]]'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das rnam par rtog pa mi 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid mthong nas rjes su dpag na/_rnam par rtog pa mi 'byung ba la ltos te/_de ma mchis zhes bgyi bar 'gyur ro/_/bcom 
[\[75b.2\]]ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po rnam par rtog pa mi 'byung ba las ltos te de med pa ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_de ni rnam par rtog pa 'byung ba'i rgyu ste/_blo gros chen po rnam par rtog pa ni de'i rwa las brten te 'byung ba'o/_/blo gros chen po 'di ltar rwa las brten 
[\[75b.3\]]te rnam par rtog pa byung bas de'i phyir brten pa'i rgyu yin pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_tha dad pa ma yin par rnam par spangs pa'i phyir de la ltos te ri bong gi rwa med pa ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po ri bong gi rwa dang rnam par rtog pa gal te tha dad na ni rwa'i rgyu las byung ba ma yin par 'gyur ro/_/de ste 
[\[75b.4\]]tha dad pa ma yin na ni de'i rgyu las byung bas rdul phra rab kyi bar du rab tu brtags na mi dmigs pas rwa dang tha dad pa ma yin pa'i phyir de'ang med par 'gyur te/_de gnyis ka'i dngos po med na gang gis ci las ltos te mos par 'gyur/_blo gros chen po gal te ltos nas med par mi 'gyur na/_
[\[75b.5\]]rgyu mi 'thun pa'i phyir ba lang gi rwa yod pa las ltos te/_ri bong gi rwa med ces kyang brtag par bya bar mi 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po rgyu mi mnyam pa'i phyir yod pa dang /_med par smra ba rnams kyi yod pa dang /_med par 'grub par mi 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang mu stegs 
[\[75b.6\]]byed pa'i lta ba can gzugs dang rgyu dang dbyibs la mngon par chags pas mngon par zhen pa/_nam mkha'i dngos po yongs su rtog pa la mi mkhas pa rnams gzugs nam mkha'i dngos po dang bral ba la bye brag 'byed par lta bas rnam par rtog ste/_blo gros chen po gzugs rnams kyi 
[\[75b.7\]]'byung ba'i rjes su zhugs pa'i phyir nam mkha' nyid kyang gzugs so/_/gzugs nyid kyang nam mkha' ste/_blo gros chen po gzugs dang nam mkha'i rgyu rnam pa tha dad pa ni gzhag pa dang gzhi'i tshul du gyur pas yid ches par bya'o/_/blo gros chen po 'byung ba rnams skye ba ni phan tshun 
[\[76a\]]
[\[76a.1\]]du rang gi mtshan nyid kyi bye brag gis tha dad pa/_nam mkha' la yang mi gnas pa yin te/_de dag la'ang nam mkha' med do/_/blo gros chen po de bzhin du ri bong gi rwa ni ba lang gi rwa las ltos pa yin te/_ba lang gi rwa'ang rdul phra mor rnam par phye ste rdul phra mo rnams 
[\[76a.2\]]kyang rnam par gzhig na rdul phra mo'i mtshan nyid la'ang mi gnas na de ci las ltos te med par 'gyur/_de ste gzhan las ltos pa'i dngos po zhig na yang de yang de lta bu'i chos can no/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/
[\[76a.3\]]blo gros chen po khyod dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan rnams kyis ri bong dang ba lang gi rwa dang /_nam mkha' dang /_gzugs su lta ba'i rnam par rtog pa dang bral ba dang /_blo gros chen po khyod kyis rang gi sems snang ba la rnam par rtog pa rjes su rtogs pa'i yid du'ang 'gyur bar bya'o/_/
[\[76a.4\]]rgyal ba'i sras dang zhing gi dkyil 'khor thams cad du'ang khyod kyis rang gi sems snang ba'i rnal 'byor bstan par bya'o/_/de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/sna tshogs snang ba yod pa min/_/snang ba las ni sems 'byung ste/_/lus 
[\[76a.5\]]dang longs spyod gnas 'dra ba'i/_/kun gzhi mi la snang ba yin/_/sems dang yid dang rnam par shes/_/rang bzhin dang ni chos lnga dang /_/bdag med gnyis po dag pa yang /_/rnam par 'dren pa rab tu gsung /_/phan tshun du ni 'brel ba las/_/ring dang thung la sogs pa 
[\[76a.6\]]'byung /_/yod pa sgrub pa med pa ste/_/med pa sgrub pa yod pa'o/_/rdul phran du ni rdzas gzhig ste/_/gzugs la rnam par brtag mi bya/_/sems tsam rnam par gzhag pa ni/_/lta ba ngan pas gsal mi 'gyur/_/mgon po rnams ni gang gsung ba'i/_/so so rang rig spyod 
[\[76a.7\]]yul te/_/rtog pa can gyi yul ma yin/_/nyan thos rnams kyi'ang de bzhin min/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos rang gi sems snang ba'i rgyud rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir yang gsol ba btab pa/_bcom ldan 'das rang 
[\[76b\]]
[\[76b.1\]]gi sems snang ba'i rgyud cig car ram rim gyis 'jug pas sam ji ltar rnam par dag par 'gyur/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po rang gi sems snang ba'i rgyud ni rim gyis 'jug pas rnam par 'dag ste/_cig car ni ma yin no/_/'di lta ste blo gros chen po 
[\[76b.2\]]shing a mra'i 'bras bu ni rim gyis smin par 'gyur gyi cig car ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po de bzhin du sems can rnams kyi rang gi sems snang ba'i rgyud kyang rim gyis rnam par 'dag ste/_cig car ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste rdza mkhan ni snod rnams rim gyis byed de 
[\[76b.3\]]cig car ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa yang sems can rnams kyi rang gi sems snang ba'i rgyud rim gyis rnam par sbyong ste cig car ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_sa chen po las rtswa dang /_shing gel pa dang /_sman dang nags tshal rnams 
[\[76b.4\]]rim gyis 'jug pas skye'i cig car ma yin no/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa yang sems can rnams kyi rang gi sems snang ba'i rgyud rim gyis sbyong ste cig car ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_bzhad gad dang /_rol mo dang /_glu dang /_pi bang dang /_sil 
[\[76b.5\]]snyan dang /_ri mo dag la mkhas pa ni rim gyis 'byung gi cig car ma yin no/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa yang sems can thams cad kyi rang gi sems snang ba'i rgyud rim gyis rnam par sbyong ste cig car ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_me long gi nang du 
[\[76b.6\]]gzugs kyi gzugs brnyan snang ba thams cad rnam par rtog pa med par cig car snang ngo /_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa yang sems can thams cad kyi rang gi sems snang ba'i rgyud rnam par mi rtog cing snang ba med pa'i spyod yul rnams cig car rnam par sbyong ngo /_/blo 
[\[76b.7\]]gros chen po 'di lta ste zla ba dang nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni 'od zer gyis gzugs su snang ba thams cad cig car mngon par byed do/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa yang sems can rang gi sems snang ba'i gnas ngan len gyi bag chags dang bral ba rnams la rgyal ba 
[\[77a\]]
[\[77a.1\]]ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i yul dang /_spyod yul cig car yang dag par ston to/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_kun gzhi rnam par shes pa ni rang gi sems snang ba'i lus dang /_gnas dang /_longs spyod kyi yul cig car rnam par ston to/_/de bzhin 
[\[77a.2\]]du blo gros chen po rgyu 'dra ba'i sangs rgyas kyang cig car sems can gyi spyod yul yongs su smin par byas te/_'og min gyi pho brang dang /_gzhal med khang gi gnas kyi rnal 'byor gyi rnal 'byor can du gzhag go/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_chos nyid kyi sangs rgyas ni cig car rgyu 
[\[77a.3\]]'dra ba dang /_sprul pa'i 'od zer gyis rnam par mdzes so/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po 'phags pa so so rang gi rig pa'i chos kyi mtshan nyid kyang yod pa dang /_med pa'i lta ba ngan pa rnam par zlog pas cig car rnam par mdzes so/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po chos nyid dang 
[\[77a.4\]]'dra bar 'byung ba'i sangs rgyas ni sgyu ma'i yan lag rnam pa mang po'i skyes bu de'i bdag nyid ma yin pa sna tshogs la mngon par zhen pa/_mi dmigs pa'i phyir rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid du gtogs pa'i chos thams cad rang gi sems snang ba'i bag chags rgyu'i mtshan nyid dang 'brel ba 
[\[77a.5\]]yongs su brtags pa'i rang bzhin la mngon par zhen pa'i rgyu las byung bar ston to/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang yongs su brtags pa'i rang bzhin 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid ni/_gzhan gyi dbang gi rang bzhin la mngon par zhen pa las 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_rtswa 
[\[77a.6\]]dang shing dang gyo mo la brten te sgyu ma'i sngags dang /_mir ldan pa las sems can thams cad kyi gzugs kyi yan lag phun sum tshogs shing sna tshogs kyi gzugs 'chang ba'i sgyu ma'i skyes bu'i gzugs sems can gyi lus rang bzhin gcig tu ma grub pa/_rnam par rtog pa rnam 
[\[77a.7\]]pa sna tshogs kyis rnam par brtags pa snang ste/_blo gros chen po de ltar snang yang de'i bdag nyid ma yin no/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po gzhan gyi dbang gi rang bzhin la kun brtags pa'i rang bzhin rnam par rtog pa'i sems sna tshogs rnam pa mang po'i mtshan nyid du snang ngo /_/
[\[77b\]]
[\[77b.1\]]blo gros chen po dngos po yongs su rtog pa'i mtshan nyid du mngon par zhen pa'i bag chags la yongs su rtog pas kun brtags pa'i rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid du 'gyur te/_blo gros chen po 'di ni 'dra bar byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi bshad pa'o/_/blo gros chen po chos nyid kyi sangs rgyas 
[\[77b.2\]]ni sems kyi rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid dang bral ba/_'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i spyod yul rnam par gzhog go/_/blo gros chen po 'phrul pas sprul pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni sbyin pa dang /_bsam gtan dang /_tshul khrims dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_sems dang /_
[\[77b.3\]]shes rab dang /_ye shes dang /_phung po dang khams dang skye mched dang rnam par thar pa dang /_rnam par shes pa 'jug pa'i mtshan nyid rab tu phye ba rgyu ba rnam par gzhag go/_/mu stegs can gyi lta ba bas gzugs med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi rim pa'i mtshan nyid kyang ston to/_/blo 
[\[77b.4\]]gros chen po chos nyid kyi sangs rgyas ni dmigs pa med cing dmigs pa dang bral ba/_bya ba dang /_dbang po dang /_tshad ma'i mtshan nyid thams cad las rnam par log pa/_byis pa dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs can bdag gi mtshan nyid la mngon par 
[\[77b.5\]]chags pas mngon par zhen pa rnams kyi yul ma yin par ston to/_/blo gros chen po de bas na 'phags pa so so rang gi rig pa'i khyad par gyi mtshan nyid la brtson par bya'o/_/khyod kyis rang gi sems kyi mtshan nyid snang ba'ang rnam par log par lta bar gyis shig_/blo gros chen 
[\[77b.6\]]po gzhan yang nyan thos kyi theg pa'i tshul rab tu dbye ba'i mtshan nyid ni rnam pa gnyis so/_/'di lta ste/_'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i khyad par gyi mtshan nyid dang /_dngos por rnam par rtog pa'i rang bzhin la mngon par zhen pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/blo gros chen po de la nyan 
[\[77b.7\]]thos rnams kyi 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i khyad par gyi mtshan nyid gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_stong pa nyid dang /_bdag med pa dang /_sdug bsngal ba dang /_mi rtag pa'i yul gyi bde ba las skyo ba dang /_rgyun chad pas phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched dang /_rang dang 
[\[78a\]]
[\[78a.1\]]spyi'i phyi rol rnam par 'jig pa med pa'i mtshan nyid yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin yongs su shes pas sems mnyam par gzhog ste/_blo gros chen po rang gi sems mnyam par bzhag nas nyan thos rnams bsam gtan dang rnam par thar pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang lam dang 'bras 
[\[78a.2\]]bu dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa dang /_rnam par grol ba dang /_bag chags bsam gyis mi khyab pa yongs su gyur pa shi 'pho dang bral ba/_'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i bde ba la gnas pa lhag par rtogs par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po 'di ni nyan thos rnams kyi 
[\[78a.3\]]'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/blo gros chen po nyan thos rnams kyi 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i gnas kyi bde ba khong du chud nas/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos sngon sems can rnams la snying brtse bar bltas te/_bdag gi smon 
[\[78a.4\]]lam mngon par bsgrubs pa'i phyir 'gog pa'i sgo'i bde ba dang /_mnyam par gzhag pa'i bde ba ni mngon du mi bya'o/_/blo gros chen po 'di ni nyan thos rnams kyi 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i mtshan nyid kyi bde ba ste/_'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i mtshan nyid kyi 
[\[78a.5\]]bde ba de la byang chub sems dpas bslab par mi bya'o/_/blo gros chen po yang nyan thos rnams kyi dngos po rnam par rtog pa'i rang bzhin la mngon par zhen pa gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_sngon po dang /_ser po dang /_dro ba dang /_gsher ba dang /_g.yo ba dang /_sra ba'i 'byung ba chen po rnams

[\[79b\]]
[\[79b.1\]]pa'i 'jigs pas skrag pa dag mya ngan las 'das pa tshol te/_blo gros chen po dngos po la rnam par rtog pa thams cad med pa dang /_dbang po rnams kyi yul ma 'ongs pa rgyun chad pas mya ngan las 'da' bar rnam par rtog gi_/blo gros chen po so so rang gis rig pa'i ye shes kun gzhir

[\[80b.1\]]par rtogs par 'gyur ba'i rigs ni gang gcig pu'i phyir mngon par rtogs pa bstan pa na mchi ma dang /_snying yer zhes lus kyi spu ldang bar 'gyur zhing 'du 'dzi med pa'i rkyen gyis dngos po la mngon par zhen pa dang /_rang gi lus kyi rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs rnam pa mang po bsnor ba dang /_zung gi cho 'phrul

[\[81b.1\]]pa rnams kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis brgya la gal te na dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su sbyong bar 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi ni sems can thams cad yongs su ma btang ngo /_/blo gros chen po de ltar de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa'i 'dod chen pa rnams

[\[81b.7\]]pa'i ye shes kyi spyod yul te/_blo gros chen po yongs su grub pa'i rang bzhin 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi snying po thugs so/_/de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis tshigs su bcad pa 'di gsungs so/_/ming dang mtshan ma kun rtog pa/_/rang bzhin gnyis kyi mtshan nyid de/_/yang 
[\[82a\]]
[\[82a.1\]]dag shes dang de bzhin nyid/_/yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/blo gros chen po 'di ni chos lnga'i rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid rab tu 'byed pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i ye shes kyi spyod yul te de la khyod dang byang chub sems dpa'

[\[82a.3\]]la sogs pa 'dzin pa la mngon par zhen pa'i phyir dbang po thams cad las rnam par shes pa 'byung ba ni 'di lta ste/_mi shes pa dang /_las dang sred pa las byung ba'i phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched bdag dang bdag gi ba med pa rang gi sems snang ba'i snod dang lus dang gzhir bdag 
[\[82a.4\]]gi sems kyis brtags pa rnams shes par byed de/_'bab chu dang /_sa bon dang /_mar me dang /_rlung dang /_sprin lta bu skad cig ma gcig nas gcig tu tha dad pas so sor gyur pa g.yo ba spre'u dang sbrang ma lta bu yul mi gtsang ba la spyod pa mgon med pa me ltar chog mi shes pa thog ma med 
[\[82a.5\]]pa'i dus nas spros pa'i yul gyi bag chags kyi rgyu las byung ba zo ba chu skor gyi 'khrul 'khor gyi 'khor lo ltar 'khor ba srid pa'i 'gro ba'i 'khor lo la lus rnam pa sna tshogs kyi gzugs 'chang ba sgyu ma dang ro langs dang 'khrul 'khor lta bur 'jug cing 'byung ste/_blo gros chen po gang mtshan nyid

[\[82a.7\]]ji ltar phung po dang /_khams dang skye mched rnams bdag la [{]stsogs [,]sogs [}]pa dang bral zhing phung po 'dus pa tsam ste las dang sred pa'i rgyu'i srad bus sbrel ba phan tshun rkyen du gyur pas g.yo ba med par 'byung ba de bzhin du/_blo gros chen po phung po dag rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid dang yang bral ba ste/_
[\[82b\]]
[\[82b.1\]]yang dag pa ma yin par kun brtags pa'i mtshan nyid kyi rnam par rtog pas rab tu phye ba la byis pa rnams rnam par rtog gi 'phags pa rnams kyis ni ma yin te/_blo gros chen po chos thams cad la sems dang yid dang rnam par shes pa dang chos lnga dang rang bzhin dang bral bar rnam par 
[\[82b.2\]]sgom pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni chos la bdag med pa la mkhas pa yin no/_/blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos bdag med pa la mkhas pa ni snang ba med pa rab tu rtogs pa'i phyir ring po mi thogs par byang chub sems dpa'i 
[\[82b.3\]]sa dang po thob ste/_sa'i mtshan nyid rab tu dbye ba khong du chud pa'i phyir rab tu dga' ba'i 'og tu sa dgu la yang mkhas par byas nas chos kyi sprin rab tu thob bo//_//de de la gnas nas sgyu ma'i rang bzhin gyi spyod yul la rab tu 'byed pas mngon par grub pa dang /_pad mo lta bu'i gzhal 
[\[82b.4\]]med khang chen por rin po che'i pad mo'i rgyal po chen po rin po che dang /_mu tig du mas rab tu brgyan pa la 'dug ste/_de dang 'thun pa'i rgyal ba'i sras rnams kyis yongs su bskor cing sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad nas byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi phyag gi dbang bskur bas 'khor los 
[\[82b.5\]]sgyur ba'i bu bzhin du mngon par dbang bskur ro/_/sangs rgyas kyi sras kyi sa las 'das nas 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i chos khong du chud pa'i phyir chos la bdag med pa mthong bas chos kyi sku la mnga' mdzad pa'i de bzhin gshegs par 'gyur te/_blo gros chen po 'di ni rang 
[\[82b.6\]]gi chos la bdag med pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/blo gros chen po de la khyod dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan dag gis kyang bslab par bya'o//_//'phags pa lang kar gshegs pa/_bam po gsum pa/_de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems 
[\[82b.7\]]dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das ji ltar bdag dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan sgro 'dogs pa dang skur pa'i lta ba ngan pa rnam par spangs pa'i blo can rnams myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang 
[\[83a\]]
[\[83a.1\]]rgya zhing mngon par sangs rgyas nas kyang rtag pa dang /_sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa dang chad par lta ba rnam par spangs te/_khyod kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul la mi skur bar 'gyur bar sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa'i mtshan nyid bdag la bshad du gsol/_de nas bcom 
[\[83a.2\]]ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos yang gsol ba btab par thugs su chud nas tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/sgro 'dogs pa dang skur pa dag_/sems tsam la ni yod pa min/_/lus dang longs spyod gnas lta bu'i/_/
[\[83a.3\]]sems ni su dag mi shes pa/_/sgro 'dogs pa dang skur pa la/_/mi mkhas de dag rab tu spyod/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshigs su bcad pa 'di nyid kyi don bstan pa'i phyir yang 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo gros chen po med pa la sgro 'dogs pa ni rnam pa bzhi ste/_
[\[83a.4\]]rnam pa bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste med pa'i mtshan nyid la sgro 'dogs pa dang /_med pa'i lta bar sgro 'dogs pa dang /_med pa'i rgyur sgro 'dogs pa dang /_med pa'i dngos por sgro 'dogs pa ste/_blo gros chen po 'di ni sgro 'dogs pa rnam pa bzhi'o/_/blo gros chen po skur pa gang zhe na/_
[\[83a.5\]]'di lta ste/_sgro 'dogs pa'i lta ba ngan pa 'di nyid mi dmigs par rab tu 'byed pa med pa'i phyir skur bar 'gyur te/_blo gros chen po 'di ni sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang med pa'i mtshan nyid la sgro 'dogs pa'i mtshan nyid gang zhe na/_'di lta 
[\[83a.6\]]ste/_phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched rnams kyi rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid la 'di ni 'di lta ste/_de bzhin nyid kyi gzhan du ma yin no zhes mngon par zhen pa'o/_/blo gros chen po 'di ni med pa'i mtshan nyid la sgro 'dogs pa'i mtshan nyid de/_blo gros chen po med pa'i mtshan nyid la sgro 
[\[83a.7\]]'dogs pa'i rnam par rtog pa 'di ni thog ma med pa'i dus kyi spros pa gnas ngan len gyi bag chags sna tshogs la mngon par zhen pa las 'byung ste/_blo gros chen po 'di ni med pa'i mtshan nyid la sgro 'dogs pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/blo gros chen po med pa'i lta bar sgro 'dogs pa ni gang phung po dang /_

[\[83b.2\]]'di lta ste/_sngon rgyu las ma skyes pa'i rnam par shes pa phyis sgyu ma lta bur sngon ma byung zhing med pa las mig dang /_gzugs dang /_snang ba'i rgyu dang /_dran pa sngon du 'jug pa las 'byung ngo /_/med pa las byung nas kyang yang 'jig par 'gyur te/_blo gros chen po 'di ni med pa'i rgyur 
[\[83b.3\]]sgro 'dogs pa'o/_/blo gros chen po med pa'i dngos por sgro 'dogs pa ni 'di lta ste/_nam mkha' dang /_'gog pa dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa ma byas pa'i dngos por mngon par zhen cing sgro 'dogs pa'o/_/blo gros chen po chos 'di dag thams cad ni dngos po dang /_dngos po med 
[\[83b.4\]]pa las rnam par log pa/_ri bong dang /_rta dang /_bong bu dang /_rnga mo'i rwa dang /_skra shad 'dzings pa lta bu yod pa dang /_med pa'i phyogs dang bral ba ste/_blo gros chen po byis pa rang gi sems snang ba tsam du ma rtogs pa'i blo can rnams sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pas rnam par rtog 
[\[83b.5\]]ste/_'phags pa rnams kyis ni ma yin no/_/'di ni blo gros chen po dngos po med par rnam par rtog pas sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/de bas na bla gros chen po sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa'i lta ba dang bral bar bya'o/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang byang chub sems 
[\[83b.6\]]dpa' rnams ni sems dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang /_chos lnga dang /_rang bzhin dang /_bdag med pa gnyis kyi mtshan nyid khong du chud par rtogs pas gzhan la phan pa'i phyir gzugs dang /_cha byad du 'dzin pa yin no/_/gzhan gyi dbang la gnas pa kun 
[\[83b.7\]]brtags pa'i rang bzhin ltar sna tshogs kyi gzugs can yid bzhin gyi nor bu lta bu sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor du dong ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad las rmi lam dang /_sgyu ma dang /_mig yor dang /_gzugs brnyan dang /_chu'i zla ba'i tshul 'dra ba'i chos skye 
[\[84a\]]
[\[84a.1\]]ba dang /_'jig pa dang /_rtag pa dang /_chad pa dang bral ba thams cad dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi theg pa dang bral ba'i chos bstan pa dag mngon du nyan cing ting nge 'dzin brgya stong rab tu thob pa nas/_ting nge 'dzin bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong gi bar du

[\[84a.7\]]sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo gros chen po de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang ngas khyod la bshad do/_/legs so bcom ldan 'das zhes gsol nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po

[\[84b.4\]]kyis stong ngo /_/blo gros chen po 'dus pa phan tshun du ltos pa dang rab tu dbye ba'i khyad par med pa'i phyir rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid du 'jug pa med de/_blo gros chen po bdag dang pha rol dang gnyis ka med pa'i phyir mtshan nyid gnas pa med pas na de'i phyir dngos po thams cad mtshan nyid stong pa zhes bya'o/_/

[\[87a\]]
[\[87a.1\]]stsal pa/_blo gros chen po chos bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams rnal 'byor chen po'i rnal 'byor can du 'gyur ro/_/bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rang gi sems snang ba rnam par sgom pa dang /_skye ba dang /_gnas pa dang /_
[\[87a.2\]]'jig pa'i lta ba rnam par spong ba dang /_phyi rol gyi dngos po dngos po med par rtogs pa dang /_'phags pa so so rang gi ye shes khong du chud par mos pa yin te/_blo gros chen po chos bzhi po 'di dag dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams rnal 'byor chen po'i 
[\[87a.3\]]rnal 'byor can du 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rang gi sems snang ba sgom pa la mkhas pa yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_'di ltar khams gsum 'di ni rang gi sems tsam ste/_bdag dang bdag gi dang bral ba g.yo ba med pa/_blang ba 
[\[87a.4\]]dang dor ba dang bral ba/_thog ma med pa'i dus kyi spros pa'i gnas ngan len gyi bag chags la mngon par zhen pas bsgos pa/_khams gsum gyi gzugs rnam pa sna tshogs la nye bar spyod pas 'brel ba/_lus dang /_longs spyod dang /_gnas dang /_'gro bar rnam par rtog 
[\[87a.5\]]pa'i rjes su song bar snang ngo /_/zhes rab tu rtog ste/_blo gros chen po de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rang gi sems snang ba rnam par sgom pa la mkhas pa yin no/_/blo gros chen po ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po skye ba dang /_gnas pa dang /_
[\[87a.6\]]'jig pa'i lta ba rnam par spangs pa yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_dngos po thams cad ni sgyu ma dang /_rmi lam gyi gzugs skye ba dang 'dra bar bdag dang /_gzhan dang gnyis ka med pa'i phyir mi skye ste/_rang gi sems snang ba tsam gyi rjes su 'brang ba dang /_phyi rol gyi dngos po med par mthong bas 
[\[87a.7\]]rnam par shes pa rnams 'jug pa med pa dang /_rkyen rnams kyang phung po dang /_spungs pa med par mthong nas khams gsum rnam par rtog pa'i rkyen las byung bar mthong nas/_phyi nang gi chos thams cad mi dmigs shing rang bzhin med pa rjes su mthong ba'i phyir skye ba'i lta ba rnam par log 
[\[87b\]]
[\[87b.1\]]nas/_sgyu ma la sogs pa'i chos kyi rang bzhin du rjes su rtogs pas mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa rab tu 'thob ste/_sa brgyad la gnas nas sems dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang /_chos lnga dang /_rang bzhin dang /_bdag med pa gnyis rtogs pa rab tu gyur pa khong du chud 
[\[87b.2\]]pa'i phyir yid kyi lus kyang rab tu 'thob bo/_/bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du yid kyi lus zhes bgyi/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po yid kyi lus zhes bya ba ni yid ltar thogs pa med cing mgyogs par 'gro ba'i phyir yid kyi lus zhes bya ste/_blo gros chen po 'di lta 
[\[87b.3\]]ste/_yid ni ri dang /_rtsig pa dang /_'bab chu dang /_shing la sogs pa la thogs pa med par dpag tshad brgya stong du mar sngon mthong ba dang /_myong ba'i yul rjes su dran zhing rang gi sems kyi rgyun ma chad pa'i lus thogs pa med pa'i 'gros kyis 'jug go/_/blo gros chen po de bzhin du yid kyi 
[\[87b.4\]]lus kyang thob ma thag tu sgyu ma dang 'dra ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis stobs dang /_dbang dang /_mngon par shes pa dang /_ye shes dang /_mtshan nyid kyi me tog rgyas pa 'phags pa'i ris su lhan cig skyes pa 'gro ba la thogs pa med par sngon gyi smon lam gyi yul rjes su dran zhing sems can yongs su smin par 
[\[87b.5\]]bya ba'i phyir yid bzhin du 'jug ste/_de ltar na blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po skye ba dang /_gnas pa dang /_'jig pa'i lta ba rnam par spangs pa yin no/_/blo gros chen po de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyi rol gyi dngos po dngos po med par 
[\[87b.6\]]rtogs pa la mkhas pa yin zhe na/_blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_dngos po thams cad ni smig rgyu dang /_rmi lam dang /_skra shad 'dzings pa lta bu'o/_/dngos po thams cad ni thog ma med pa'i dus kyi spros pa'i gnas ngan len gyi rnam par rtog pa'i bag chags rnam pa sna tshogs la mngon par 
[\[87b.7\]]zhen pa'i rgyu las byung ba yin te/_rang bzhin med pa'o zhes yang dag par mthong nas 'phags pa so so rang gi ye shes kyis rtogs pa'i yul la mngon par mos so/_/blo gros chen po chos 'di bzhi dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni rnal 'byor chen po'i rnal 'byor can yin no/_/
[\[88a\]]
[\[88a.1\]]blo gros chen po de la khyod kyis brtson par gyis shig_/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos gsol ba btab pa/_bcom ldan 'das rgyu dang rkyen gyi mtshan nyid rtogs pa gang gis bdag dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan 
[\[88a.2\]]rnams mchis pa dang /_ma mchis par lta ba'i rnam par rtog pa dang bral zhing dngos po thams cad rim gyis dang /_cig car skye bar rnam par mi rtog par mi 'gyur ba'i chos thams cad kyi rgyu dang /_rkyen gyi mtshan nyid bdag la bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo 
[\[88a.3\]]gros chen po chos thams cad kyi rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid rnam pa gnyis te/_'di ltar phyi'i dang /_nang gi'o/_/blo gros chen po de la phyi'i rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba ni 'jim pa'i gong bu dang /_lag zungs dang /_'khor lo dang /_srad bu dang /_chu dang /_mi'i nan tan 
[\[88a.4\]]la sogs pa'i rkyen rnams kyis rdza ma 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po ji ltar 'jim pa'i gong bu las rdza ma 'byung ba de bzhin du thags las snam bu dang /_rtsi skyang las re lde dang /_sa bon las myu gu dang /_srub ma la sogs pa mi'i nan tan dang /_zho las mar 'byung ba 'di ni blo gros chen po phyi'i rten 
[\[88a.5\]]cing 'brel par 'byung ba snga ma snga ma las phyi ma phyi mar 'byung ba'o/_/blo gros chen po de la nang gi rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba ni 'di lta ste/_ma rig pa dang /_sred pa dang /_las dang /_chos 'di dag la sogs pa ni rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i ming 'thob bo/_/blo gros chen po 'di 
[\[88a.6\]]dag las skyes pa'i phung po dang /_khams dang /_/skye mched ces bya ba'i chos de dag kyang rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i ming 'thob ste/_de dag ni khyad par med pa las byis pa rnams kyis brtags so/_/blo gros chen po de la rgyu ni rnam pa drug ste/_'di ltar 'byung bar 'gyur ba'i rgyu 
[\[88a.7\]]dang /_'brel ba'i rgyu dang /_mtshan nyid kyi rgyu dang /_byed pa'i rgyu dang /_snang bar byed pa'i rgyu dang /_ltos pa'i rgyu dang drug go/_/de la blo gros chen po 'byung bar 'gyur ba'i rgyu ni phyi nang gi chos rnams skye ba'i phyir rgyu'i bya ba byed do/_/blo gros chen po 'brel ba'i rgyu ni phyi nang gi phung po dang /_sa bon 
[\[88b\]]
[\[88b.1\]]la sogs pa 'byung ba'i dmigs pa'i bya ba byed do/_/blo gros chen po mtshan nyid kyi rgyu ni de ma thag tu byed pa'i mtshan nyid dang 'brel ba skyed do/_/blo gros chen po byed pa'i rgyu ni dbang bar gtogs pa'i bya ba byed de/_'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po bzhin no/_/blo gros chen po snang bar byed pa'i rgyu ni 
[\[88b.2\]]rnam par rtog pa'i dngos po skyes pa'i mtshan nyid snang ba'i bya ba byed de/_gzugs la sogs pa la mar me bzhin no/_/blo gros chen po ltos pa'i rgyu ni rnam par ldog pa'i dus na rnam par mi rtog pa'i dngos po 'byung ba'i phyir rgyun gyi bya ba mi 'chad par byed do/_/'di dag ni blo gros chen po byis pa so so'i 
[\[88b.3\]]skye bo rnams kyis rang gi sems kyi rnam par rtog pas brtags pa ste/_rim gyis 'jug pas kyang ma yin no/_/cig car yang mi 'byung ngo /_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po gal te cig car 'byung na ni rgyu'i mtshan nyid du ma red pa'i phyir bya ba dang byed pa'i bye brag kyang med par 'gyur ro/_/de ste rim 
[\[88b.4\]]gyis 'jug pas 'byung na ni mtshan nyid du ma red pa'i bdag nyid las rim gyis 'jug par mi 'byung ste/_blo gros chen po bu ma skyes par pha zhes gdags pa dang 'dra bar rtog pa pa rnams kyi rim gyis 'jug pas 'brel pa'i tshul yang mi rung ngo /_/blo gros chen po rgyu dang /_dmigs pa dang /_de ma thag 
[\[88b.5\]]pa dang /_bdag po'i rkyen la sogs pas bskyed par bya ba dang /_skyed pa'i phyir rim gyis 'jug pas mi skye ste/_blo gros chen po kun brtags pa'i rang bzhin la mngon par zhen pa'i mtshan nyid kyi phyir rim gyis sam cig car mi skye'o/_/blo gros chen po rang gi sems snang ba'i lus dang /_
[\[88b.6\]]longs spyod can yin pa'i phyir rang dang /_spyi'i mtshan nyid phyi rol gyi dngos po med pa'i phyir rim gyis sam cig car yang mi skye ste/_gzhan du na rang gi sems snang ba'i rnam par rtog pas rnam par brtags pa'i phyir rnam par shes pa 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po de lta bas na khyod kyis rgyu dang rkyen 
[\[88b.7\]]dang /_byed pa dang ldan pa'i mtshan nyid rim pa'am cig car du lta ba dang bral bar bya'o/_/de la 'di skad ces bya ste/_'di la gang yang rkyen rnams kyis/_/skye ba med cing 'gag pa med/_/btags pa'i rkyen rnams kho na ni/_/skye zhing 'gag par 'gyur yin no/_/skye 'jig rkyen la 'brel 
[\[89a\]]
[\[89a.1\]]ba ni/_/rnam par bzlog par mi bya ste/_/byis pa rkyen gyis gang rtog pa/_/de ni rnam par bzlog par bya/_/bag chags rnams kyis rmongs byas sems/_/srid gsum dag na snang ba'i phyir/_/rkyen rnams yod dang med las kyang /_/chos rnams 'byung ba med 
[\[89a.2\]]pa'o/_/ma byung rkyen gyis skye ba med/_/gang yang 'gag par mi 'gyur te/_/nam mkha'i me tog mo sham bur/_/gang tshe 'dus byas mthong ba na/_/de tshe gzung dang 'dzin pa yang /_/nor bar mthong bar phyir ldog go/_/skyes med bskyed par bya ba med/_/rkyen rnams dag ni gang 
[\[89a.3\]]dag kyang /_/gag kyang gang tshe'ang yod pa min/_/spyod pa dag tu brjod pa yin/_/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid kyi snying po tha dad pa'i bye 
[\[89a.4\]]brag shin tu rtogs pa gang gis bdag dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzhan rnams brjod pa dang /_brjod du ma mchis pa'i don gnyis rtogs par khong du chud nas myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/_sems can 
[\[89a.5\]]thams cad kyi brjod pa dang /_brjod du ma mchis pa'i don gnyis kyi rtogs pa rnam par sbyong bar 'gyur ba tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid kyi snying po zhes bgyi ba'i chos kyi gzhung bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen 
[\[89a.6\]]po de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang ngas khyod la bshad do/_/bcom ldan 'das legs so zhes gsol nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po bcom ldan 'das la phyir nyan to/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo 
[\[89a.7\]]gros chen po tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid rnam pa bzhi yod de/_'di ltar mtshan nyid kyi tshig dang /_rmi lam gyi tshig dang /_gnas ngan len la mngon par zhen pa'i tshig dang /_thog ma med pa'i dus kyi rnam par rtog pa'i tshig go/_/blo gros chen po de la mtshan nyid kyi tshig ni rang gi rnam 
[\[89b\]]
[\[89b.1\]]par rtog pa'i gzugs la mtshan mar mngon par zhen pa las 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po rmi lam gyi tshig ni sngon myong ba'i yul rjes su dran pa dang rtogs pa'i yul med pa las 'byung ngo /_/yang blo gros chen po gnas ngan len gyi rnam par rtog pa'i tshig ni sngon dgras byas pa'i las rjes su dran pa las 'byung 
[\[89b.2\]]ngo /_/blo gros chen po thog ma med pa'i dus nas rnam par rtog pa'i tshig ni thog ma med pa'i dus kyi spros pa la mngon par zhen pa'i gnas ngan len gyi rang gi sa bon las 'byung ste/_'di ni blo gros chen po tshig rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid rnam pa bzhi'o/_/zhes gang brjod pa de la phyir bshad pa'o/_/
[\[89b.3\]]de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos don de nyid kyi phyir gsol ba btab pa/_bcom ldan 'das da dung yang bcom ldan 'das kyis mi rnams kyis tshig rnam par rig pa rtog pa gang du ci las ji ltar gang gis 'byung ba'i tshig gi rnam par rtog 
[\[89b.4\]]pa mngon par zhen pa'i spyod yul bdag la bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po tshig 'byung ba ni mgo dang brang dang /_sna dang mgul dang /_rkan dang mchu dang /_lce dang so rnams 'dus pa las 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen pos gsol pa/_bcom ldan 
[\[89b.5\]]'das rnam par rtog pa dang tshig tu tha dad dam/_'on te gcig gam ci lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po tshig ni rnam par rtog pa dang /_tha dad pa'ang ma yin gcig pa'ang ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_de'i rgyu las byung ba'i mtshan nyid kyi 
[\[89b.6\]]phyir tshig gi rnam par rtog par 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen po de ste tshig 'di rnam par rtog pa dang tha dad pa zhig yin na ni/_rnam par rtog pa'i rgyu las byung ba ma yin par 'gyur ro/_/de ste gcig pa zhig yin na ni tshig de don gsal bar byed par yang mi 'gyur na/_de ni byed de de bas na tha dad 
[\[89b.7\]]pa'ang ma yin/_gcig pa'ang ma yin no/_/yang blo gros chen pos gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das tshig nyid don dam pa lags sam/_'on te tshig gis gang brjod par bgyi ba de don dam pa lags sam ci lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po tshig kyang don 
[\[90a\]]
[\[90a.1\]]dam pa ma yin no/_/tshig gis gang brjod par bya ba'ang don dam pa ma yin te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_'phags pa'i ye shes don dam pa'i bden pa brjod pas don dam pa la 'jug pa'i phyir tshig nyid ni don dam pa ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po don dam pa ni 
[\[90a.2\]]'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i ye shes kyis rtogs par bya ba yin te/_tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i blo'i spyod yul ma yin no/_/de bas na rnam par rtog pa ni don dam pa rjod par mi byed do/_/blo gros chen po tshig ni skyes nas 'jig cing mi brtan pa/_phan tshun gyi rgyu dang rkyen las byung ngo /_/
[\[90a.3\]]blo gros chen po gang phan tshun gyi rgyu dang rkyen las byung ba de ni don dam par rjod par mi byed do/_/blo gros chen po bdag dang gzhan gyi mtshan nyid med pa'i phyir tshig ni mtshan nyid rjod par mi byed do/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang rang gi sems snang ba tsam gyi rjes su 'brang ba dang /_phyi rol gyi dngos 
[\[90a.4\]]po rnam pa tha dad pa sna tshogs kyi mtshan nyid med pas rnam par rtog pa ni don dam pa la mi rtog go/_/de bas na blo gros chen po khyod kyis tshig rnam pa sna tshogs kyi rnam par rtog pa dang bral bar bya'o/_/de la 'di skad ces bya ste/_dngos po kun gyi rang bzhin med/_/tshig kyang de bzhin med pa ste/_/
[\[90a.5\]]stong pa nyid dam stong nyid don/_/byis pas ma mthong rnam par rgyug_/dngos po thams cad rang bzhin med/_/mi yi tshig kyang brtags pa ste/_/de yang yod pa ma yin no/_/snying po rmi lam sprul 'dra bar/_/yongs su brtags par byas pa ni/_/mi 'khor mya ngan 'da' mi bya/_/ji ltar 
[\[90a.6\]]rgyal po tshong dpon dag_/'ji ba'i [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]sna tshogs kyis/_/bu rnams brid de rtser bcug cing /_/de nas [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dngos byin pa/_/de bzhin ngas kyang chos rnams kyi/_mtshan nyid sna tshogs gzugs brnyan gyis/_/so so rang rig yang dag mtha'/_/bu rnams la ni ngas bshad 
[\[90a.7\]]do/_/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad kyi rang gi sems snang ba'i spyod yul rtogs pa/_rnam par phye ba'i mtshan nyid mchis pa dang /_ma mchis pa dang /_gcig pa 
[\[90b\]]
[\[90b.1\]]dang /_tha dad pa dang /_gnyis ka dang /_gnyis ka ma lags pa dang /_mchis pa'ang ma lags ma mchis pa'ang ma lags pa dang /_rtag pa dang /_mi rtag pa spangs pa/_mu stegs can gyi rtog pa mi rgyu ba/_'phags pa'i so so rang gi ye shes kyis mkhyen par bgyi ba/_kun 
[\[90b.2\]]brtags pa'i rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid las rnam par log pa/_don dam pa de kho na nyid la 'jug pa/_sa'i mtshams sbyor zhing gong nas gong du rnam par dag pa'i mtshan nyid de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la rjes su 'jug pa'i mtshan nyid sngon gyi smon lam gyis gzugs sna tshogs kyi nor bu lta 
[\[90b.3\]]bur 'jug pa/_yul mtha' yas pa'i mtshan nyid lhun gyis grub par rab tu rgyu ba 'di dag la sogs pa la ci nas kyang bdag dang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzhan rnams kun brtags pa'i rang bzhin gyi rang dang /_spyi'i mtshan nyid las rnam par log pa'i lta ba can rnams 
[\[90b.4\]]su gyur te/_myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par sangs rgyas nas/_sems can thams cad kyi bde ba phun sum tshogs pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par bgyi ba bdag la bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po 
[\[90b.5\]]legs so legs so/_/khyod don 'di gsol ba 'debs par sems pa ni blo gros chen po khyod 'gro ba mang po la phan pa dang /_'gro ba mang po la bde ba dang /_'jig rten la snying brtse ba dang /_'gro ba phal po che'i don dang lha dang mi rnams la phan pa dang bde ba'i phyir zhugs pa ste/_blo gros 
[\[90b.6\]]chen po khyod legs so/_/blo gros chen po de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang khyod la ngas bshad do/_/legs so bcom ldan 'das zhes gsol nas/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la phyir nyan to/_/bcom ldan 
[\[90b.7\]]'das kyis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo gros chen po byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams kyis rang gi sems snang ba tsam du khong du ma chud pa dang /_phyi rol gyi dngos po rnam pa sna tshogs la mngon par zhen pas yod pa dang /_med pa dang /_gcig pa dang tha dad pa dang /_gnyis ka dang /_
[\[91a\]]
[\[91a.1\]]gnyis ka ma yin pa dang /_yod pa yang ma yin med pa yang ma yin pa dang /_rtag pa dang mi rtag pa'i rang bzhin gyi bag chags kyi rgyu rnam par rtog pa la mngon par zhen pas rnam par rtog go/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_smig rgyu'i chu la dpyid kyi nyi mas gdungs pa'i 
[\[91a.2\]{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rnams chu'i dngos por rnam par rtog cing btung bar 'dod pa'i phyir rab tu rgyug ste/_rang gi sems kyi lta bas nor ba khong du ma chud pa'i phyir 'di na chu med do zhes bya bar yang mi shes so/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams kyang thog ma med pa'i dus kyi rnam pa sna 
[\[91a.3\]]tshogs kyis spros pa la rnam par rtog pas bsgos pa'i blo can/_'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi mes yongs su gdungs pa'i yid can/_gzugs rnam pa sna tshogs kyi yul la mngon par mos pa/_skye ba dang /_'jig pa dang /_gnas par lta ba'i bsam pa can 
[\[91a.4\]]phyi nang gi dngos po la mi mkhas pa de dag gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_yod pa dang /_med par 'dzin par rab tu lhung ngo /_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_dri za'i grong khyer la grong khyer ma yin par mi mkhas pa rnams la grong khyer gyi 'du shes 'byung ste/_grong khyer lta bu de ni thog ma med 
[\[91a.5\]]pa'i dus nas grong khyer gyi sa bon gyi bag chags la mngon par zhen pa'i phyir snang ste/_blo gros chen po grong khyer de ni grong khyer yang ma yin/_grong khyer med pa'ang ma yin no/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po rang gi sems snang ba tsam du khong du ma chud pa'i blo can thog ma med pa'i dus nas mu stegs 
[\[91a.6\]]can spros pa'i bag chags la mngon par zhen pa rnams gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_yod pa dang /_med pa'i smra ba la mngon par chags so/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_mi la la zhig nyal ba'i rmi lam du bud med dang /_skyes pa dang /_glang po dang /_rta dang /_shing rta dang 
[\[91a.7\]]rkang thang dang /_grong dang /_grong khyer dang /_grong rdal dang /_ba lang dang /_ma he dang /_nags tshal dang /_skyed mos tshal dang /_ri dang /_'bab chu dang /_rdzing rnam pa sna tshogs kyis mdzes par byas pa'i yul grong khyer rin po cher phyin pa snyam la/_sad nas de sad pa dang yul 
[\[91b\]]
[\[91b.1\]]grong khyer rin po che nyid rjes su dran na/_blo gros chen po de ji snyam du sems/_de ltar yang dag pa ma yin pa'i rmi lam rnam pa sna tshogs rjes su dran par byed pa'i skyes bu de ci mkhas pa'i rang bzhin can yin nam/_gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis

[\[92a.1\]]yin pa'i smra ba la mngon par zhen cing dam pa'i chos la skur pa rnams bdag dang gzhan dag kyang log par ltung bar byed do/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_'khor lo ma yin yang mgal me'i 'khor lo la byis pa rnams 'khor lo'i dngos por yongs su rtog ste/_mkhas pa rnams

[\[95a.1\]]pa ma yin pa dang /_rtag pa ma yin pa dang /_don gcig pa ma yin pa dang /_don tha dad pa ma yin pa'i phyir mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams kyi mya ngan las 'das pa ni rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid khong du chud pa dang /_

[\[95b.1\]]bstan pas blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje'i snying po dang /_yon tan de lta bu'i mtshan nyid dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzhan dag la ji lta ba bzhin du byin gyi rlabs mdzad do/_/blo gros chen po de ltar sa dang po la byang chub sems

[\[95b.3\]]la pad mo chen po'i gzhal med khang du stan la 'dug pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rin po che'i rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa'i cod pan can/_de dang 'thun pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis yongs su bskor ba la rgyal ba'i phyag phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten

[\[100a.5\]]to/_/de la blo gros chen po ming gi tshogs zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_dngos po gang la gnas te ming du gdags pa'o/_/tshogs de ni dngos po dang /_tshogs dang /_lus zhes bya ba don tha mi dad de/_blo gros chen po de ni ming gi tshogs so/_/blo gros chen po tshig gi tshogs ni 'di lta ste/_tshig 
[\[100a.6\]]gi don mang po bden pa dang /_gdon mi za ba dang /_thug pa dang /_dmigs pa zhes bya ba don tha mi dad de/_blo gros chen po de ni tshig gi tshogs ngas bstan pa byas so/_/yang blo gros chen po yi ge'i tshogs ni 'di lta ste/_gang gis ming dang tshig snang bar byed pa yin te/_yi ge dang /_rtags 
[\[100a.7\]]dang /_mtshan ma dang /_dmigs pa dang /_'dogs pa zhes bya ba ni don tha mi dad do/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po tshig gi tshogs ni 'di lta ste/_tshig thug pa'o/_/blo gros chen po ming ni 'di lta ste/_yi ge rnams kyi rang gi ming tha dad pa a zhes bya ba nas ha'i bar du'o/_/de la blo gros chen 
[\[100b\]]
[\[100b.1\]]po yi ge'i tshogs ni 'di lta ste/_thung du 'dren pa dang /_ring du 'dren pa dang /_yi ge'i mjug go/_/de la blo gros chen po tshig gi tshogs ni glang po che dang /_rta dang /_mi dang /[ {]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dang /_ba lang dang /_ma he dang /_lug dang /_phyugs la sogs pa gang sa la 'gro ba rnams ni tshig gi

[\[100b.3\]]ming dang tshig brjod pa'i mtshan nyid de/_de la khyod kyis 'dris par bya'o/_/de la 'di skad ces bya ste/_tshig dang yi ge'i tshogs rnams dang /_/ming la'ang rnam pa'i bye brag tu/_/mi shes byis pa chags pa ni/_/glang po chen po rdzab song bzhin/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po ma 'ongs 
[\[100b.4\]]pa'i dus na/_mi mkhas pa la log pa'i rtog ge pa dag la mkhas pa dag gis dris te/_gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_gnyi ga dang /_gnyi ga ma yin par lta ba'i mtshan nyid las rnam par grol ba/_mtha' gnyis kyi tshul dris na/_rigs pa dang /_rgyu dang /_blo med pas 'di ni dri ba ma 
[\[100b.5\]]yin te/_tshul bzhin ma yin no zhes de skad ces zer ro/_/'di lta ste/_gzugs la sogs pa dang /_mi rtag pa gcig gam tha dad ces bya ba dang /_de bzhin du 'du byed las mya ngan las 'das pa dang /_mtshan las mtshan gyi gzhi dang /_yon tan las yon tan can dang /_'byung ba dang /_
[\[100b.6\]]'byung ba las gyur pa dang /_snang ba las mthong ba dang /_rdul las rdul phra mo dang /_ye shes las rnal 'byor can rnams dang /_de dag la sogs pa gcig nas gcig tu [{]go rims kyi [,]go rim gyi [}]mtshan nyid kyi tshul gyis lung bstan pa rnams dris na 'di ni gzhag pa ste/_bcom ldan 'das kyis lung 
[\[100b.7\]]ma bstan to zhes zer ro/_/skyes bu gti mug pa de dag ni ji ltar nyan pa rnams blo dang mi ldan pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams sems can rnams kyi skrag pa'i gnas spang ba'i phyir lung mi bstan pa rnams lung mi ston to/_/de ni blo gros chen 
[\[101a\]]
[\[101a.1\]]po mu stegs byed kyi lta ba gzhig pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis ma bshad do/_/blo gros chen po mu stegs byed ni 'di skad ces smra ste/_'di ltar lus de ni srog dang bcas pa'o/_/lus kyang gzhan srog kyang gzhan no zhes de la sogs pa lung du mi ston par smra ste/_

[\[101a.3\]]chen po gang gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa la mngon par chags shing rang gi sems snang ba tsam du mi shes pa'i blo can de dag la ni bzhag pa yod do/_/blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni tshig dris pa la lung bstan pa rnam pa 
[\[101a.4\]]bzhis sems can rnams la chos ston te/_blo gros chen po gzhag pa zhes bya ba de ni ngas dus gzhan du bstan par byas pa yin te/_dbang po yongs su ma smin pa rnams la gzhag pa yin no/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni bya ba dang byed pa med pa ste/_byed pa med pas na mi 
[\[101a.5\]]skye'o/_/de'i phyir chos thams cad ni ma skyes pa zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni ngo bo nyid med pa ste/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po 'di ltar rang gi blos rnam par dpyad na rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid kyis dngos po mi rigs pas na de'i phyir chos thams cad ngo bo nyid med pa 
[\[101a.6\]]zhes bya'o/_/de la blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni blang ba dang /_dor ba med pa yin te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po 'di ltar rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid blang na yang blang du med dor na yang dor du med do/_/des na de'i phyir blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni blang ba dang dor ba med pa'o/_/
[\[101a.7\]]yang blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni ma 'gags pa yin te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_dngos po'i ngo bo nyid dang /_mtshan nyid med pas na chos thams cad mi dmigs te/_de'i phyir chos thams cad ni ma 'gags pa zhes bya'o/_/de la yang blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni mi rtag 
[\[101b\]]
[\[101b.1\]]pa yin te/_de ci'i phyir de skad ces bya zhe na/_mtshan nyid skye ba mi rtag pa yin pas de'i phyir chos thams cad mi rtag pa zhes bya'o/_/de la yang blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni rtag pa yin te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_mtshan nyid skye ba mi skye ba'i dngos po rtag pas brtags

[\[101b.5\]]ba rab tu dbye ba'i tshul gyis mtshan nyid la mkhas pas/_gong nas gong du lan cig phyir 'ong ba dang /_phyir mi 'ong ba dang /_dgra bcom par 'gyur ba'i thabs kyi mtshan nyid kyi rnam par shes shing sems can rnams la de lta de lta bur chos bstan pa dang /_ci nas bdag med pa'i mtshan 
[\[101b.6\]]nyid gnyis dang /_sgrib pa gnyis so so rnam par sbyangs te/_sa rnams kyi sa'i mtshan nyid las 'da' ba rtogs par khong du chud pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i 'gro ba dang /_yul dang /_spyod yul rab tu rnyed nas gzugs sna tshogs kyi nor bu dang 'dra bas sems 
[\[101b.7\]]can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar 'gyur ba dang /_chos thams cad kyi yul dang /_'gro ba la 'dod pa bzhin du longs spyod pas nye bar 'tsho bar 'gyur ba'i rgyun du zhugs pa rnams kyi rgyun du chud pa'i 'gro ba rab tu dbye ba'i tshul gyi mtshan nyid bdag la bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' 
[\[102a\]]
[\[102a.1\]]stsal pa/_blo gros chen po de'i phyir legs par nyon la rab tu yid la zung shig dang ngas khyod la bshad do/_/legs so bcom ldan 'das zhes gsol nas/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po/_bcom ldan 'das la slar nyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das

[\[102b.1\]]'dzin pa med pas spangs par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po rgyun du zhugs pa'i lhan cig skyes pa'i 'jig tshogs la lta ba ni bdag dang gzhan gyi lus mnyam pa nyid dang /_phung po bzhi gzugs med pa'i mtshan nyid dang /_gzugs skye ba 'byung ba dang /_'byung ba las gyur pa dang /_'byung

[\[104b.1\]]rang gis rig pa'i bde bas mnyam par bzhag cing sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir sprul pa'i 'od zer sna tshogs kyis rnam par mdzes so/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos 'byung ba chen po dang /_'byung ba las gyur pa la mkhas par
