[\[132a.1\]]po brgya byin dbang po grong khyer 'jig dang /_lag pa byed pa spyod pa dang /_lus 'dus pa gzugs dang /_sa gnas nor 'dzin dang /_nam mkha' go 'byed 'ong rgyu zhes de la sogs pa dngos po re re la rnam grangs kyi tshig gi sgra mang po rnam par brtags pa yod de/_de 
[\[132a.2\]]dag ni ming mang ba'i phyir dngos po mang por mi rtog mod kyi/_rang bzhin ma yin par yang mi 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po nga yang mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams na ming grangs med pa brgya stong phrag gsum du byis pa rnams kyi rna lam du grag ste/_de dag gis nga brjod kyang 
[\[132a.3\]]'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i ming gi rnam grangs su shes so/_/de la blo gros chen po kha cig ni nga la de bzhin gshegs par shes so/_/la las ni rang 'byung bar/_de bzhin du la las ni 'dren pa dang /_rnam par 'dren pa dang /_yongs su 'dren pa dang /_sangs rgyas dang /_
[\[132a.4\]]khyu mchog dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jug sel dang /_dbang phyug dang /_gtso bo dang /_ser skya dang /_yang dag mu dang /_mi gnod mu khyud dang /_zla ba dang /_nyi ma dang /_rtse ba po dang /_rnam par 'dor dang /_ne tso dang /_dbang po dang /_stobs can dang /_chu bdag ces 
[\[132a.5\]]rab tu shes so/_/gzhan la las ni skye ba med pa dang /_'gog pa med pa dang /_stong pa nyid dang /_de bzhin nyid dang /_bden pa nyid dang /_yang dag pa nyid dang /_yang dag pa'i mtha' dang /_chos kyi dbyings dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa dang /_rtag pa dang /_mnyam pa 
[\[132a.6\]]nyid dang /_gnyis su med pa dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang /_rkyen las sangs rgyas su 'gyur ba'i rgyu bstan pa dang /_rnam par thar pa dang /_lam dang /_bden pa rnams dang /_thams cad mkhyen pa dang /_rgyal ba dang /_yid kyi rang bzhin no zhes rab tu shes so/_/blo gros chen 
[\[132a.7\]]po de la sogs pa ming grangs med pa brgya stong phrag gsum tshang ba lhag chad med par nga la 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dang /_gzhan dag na skye bo rnams kyis shes te/_chu'i zla ba bzhin du zhugs pa'ang med/_byung ba'ang med mod kyi/_byis pa rnams mtha' gnyis su lhung ba'i rgyud kyis khong du 
[\[132b\]]
[\[132b.1\]]chud de/_'on kyang nga la bkur sti byed/_nga la gtso bor byed/_ri mor byed/_mchod par byed de/_tshig gi don dang nges pa'i tshig la mi mkhas pas ming tha dad pa ma yin par rang gi tshul mi shes so/_/bshad pa'i sgra dang /_brjod pa la mngon par chags pas skye ba med pa dang /_
[\[132b.2\]]'gog pa med pa'i dngos po med par rtog ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i ming gi rnam grangs gzhan du dbang po dang /_brgya byin dang /_grong khyer 'jig lta bur ni mi rtog go/_/chos thams cad sgra ji bzhin pa'i don du brjod pa'i phyir 'brang bas rang gi tshul so sor gzhog pa brjod pa la mi 
[\[132b.3\]]mos so/_/blo gros chen po skyes bu blun po de dag 'di skad du don ni sgra ji bzhin pa nyid de/_sgra las don gzhan ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_don la lus med pa'i phyir sgra las don gzhan ma yin te/_sgra nyid don yin no zhes zer ro/_/blo gros chen po sgra'i rang 
[\[132b.4\]]bzhin yongs su mi shes pas mi mkhas pa'i blo can rnams ni ji ltar sgra ni skye zhing 'jig gi don ni ma skyes mi 'jig pa de ltar mi shes so/_/blo gros chen po sgra ni yi ger lhung ba'o/_/don ni yi ger ma lhung ba ste/_dngos po dang dngos po med pa rnam par spangs pa'i phyir skye ba med cing lus med 
[\[132b.5\]]do/_/blo gros chen po yi ge rnams yod pa dang /_med pa mi dmigs pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ni yi ger lhung bas chos mi ston te/_gzhan du na yi ger ma lhung bas chos ston to/_/blo gros chen po chos la yi ge med pa'i phyir gang gis yi ger lhung ba'i chos 'chad pa de 
[\[132b.6\]]ni 'khyal pa ste/_des na de'i phyir blo gros chen po bshad pa brjod pa las de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis yi ge gcig kyang ma bshad/_'chad par mi 'gyur ro zhes nga dang /_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan dag gis bstan to/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_
[\[132b.7\]]chos thams cad la yi ge med pa'i phyir don dang mi ldan par yang mi 'chad de/_rnam par rtog pa bzung nas 'chad do/_/blo gros chen po chos thams cad ma bshad na ni bstan pa 'jig par 'gyur te/_bstan pa zhig na ni sangs rgyas dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_nyan thos rnams kyang med par 
[\[133a\]]
[\[133a.1\]]'gyur ro/_/de dag med na ni su la ci zhig bshad de/_des na de'i phyir blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos bshad pa brjod pa'i sgra la mngon par ma chags par bya'o/_/blo gros chen po bshad pa'i brjod pa ni sems can rnams kyi bsam pa 
[\[133a.2\]]bzhin du zhugs pas 'khrul pa yin te/_nga dang de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gzhan rnams kyis ni sems dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa bzlog pa'i phyir mos pa tha dad pa'i sems can rnams la chos bstan par bya'i chos thams 
[\[133a.3\]]cad snang ba med pa/_rang gi sems snang ba tsam du khong du chud pa'i phyir/_rnam par rtog pa rnam pa mang po log pas bdag gi so so rang gi 'phags pa'i ye shes rtogs pa bzlog pa ni ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos don la rton par bya'i/_yi ge 
[\[133a.4\]]la rton par mi bya'o/_/blo gros chen po yi ge'i phyir 'brang ba'i rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo ni bdag kyang don las nyams par byed cing /_gzhan yang khong du chud par mi byed do/_/chos thams cad dang /_sa'i rang gi mtshan nyid la mi mkhas pa/_tshig dang nges pa'i 
[\[133a.5\]]sgra mngon par mi shes pa'i ngan par rtog ge pa rnams lta ba ngan par lhung ba'i rgyud kyis rang gi phyogs sgrogs par byed do/_/de ste chos thams cad dang /_sa'i rang gi mtshan nyid la mkhas shing /_tshig gi rnam grangs dang /_nges pa'i tshig rtogs par khong du chud de/_dngos po'i don 
[\[133a.6\]]dang /_rigs pa la mkhas par gyur na/_de'i phyir bdag nyid kyang yang dag pa mtshan ma med pa'i bde bas tshim par byed cing /_gzhan yang yang dag par theg pa chen po la 'god do/_/blo gros chen po theg pa chen po yang dag par yongs su bzung na/_sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub 
[\[133a.7\]]sems dpa' dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas rnams yongs su bzung ba byas pa yin no/_/sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas rnams yongs su bzung bas sems can rnams yongs su bzung ba byas pa yin no/_/sems can thams 
[\[133b\]]
[\[133b.1\]]cad yongs su bzung bas dam pa'i chos yongs su bzung ba yin no/_/blo gros chen po dam pa'i chos yongs su bzung bas sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi 'chad par byas pa yin no/_/sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi 'chad pa'i phyir skye gnas kyi bye brag rab tu 'thob par shes so/_/de bas na skye gnas 
[\[133b.2\]]dam pa 'thob pa de dag tu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams skye ba yongs su 'dzin cing theg pa chen po rab tu bzung ba'i phyir dbang bcus gzugs dang /_cha byad sna tshogs 'dzin par gyur te/_sems can gyi cha byad dang /_bag la nyal gyi mtshan nyid rtogs par khong du 
[\[133b.3\]]chud pas de bzhin nyid du chos ston to/_/de la de bzhin nyid dang /_gzhan ma yin pa nyid dang /_de kho na nyid dang /_blang ba med pa dang /_dor ba med pa'i mtshan nyid dang /_spros pa thams cad nye bar zhi ba ni de kho na nyid ces bya'o/_/blo gros chen po de kho na nyid la yi ge med pa'i phyir 
[\[133b.4\]]rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mos sgra ji bzhin gyi don du chags pa la mkhas par mi bya ste/_sor mo lta ba lta bur mi bya'o/_/'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po la las kha cig la sor mo'i rtse mos ci zhig bstan na/_de sor mo'i rtse mo nyid la lta zhing 'brang ba de bzhin du/_blo gros chen po 
[\[133b.5\]]byis pa so so'i skye bo'i sde rnams byis pa'i rang bzhin can lta bur sgra ji bzhin gyi sor mo'i rtse mo la mngon par chags shing zhen to/_/de ltar sgra'i sor mo'i rtse mo la mngon par chags bzhin du 'chi ba'i dus byed par 'gyur te/_sgra ji bzhin gyi sor mo'i rtse mo'i don spangs la/_don dam pa 
[\[133b.6\]]khong du chud par bya ba ni mi byed do/_/'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po byis pa rnams kyi zas dang /_skom ni g.yos ma byas par su yang za ba med de/_la la g.yos su ma byas par za na yang kha zas mthar gyis g.yos su bya ba mi shes pas de smyon par brtag par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du blo 
[\[133b.7\]]gros chen po skye ba med pa dang /_'gog pa med pa yang mngon par 'dus ma byas na mdzes par mi 'gyur gyi/_'di la gdon mi za bar mngon par 'du bya bar 'gyur te/_sor mo'i rtse mo'i don mthong ba bzhin du tshig tsam ni ma yin no/_/des na de'i phyir blo gros chen po don la brtson par bya'o/_
[\[134a\]]
[\[134a.1\]]blo gros chen po don ni rab tu dben pa ste/_mya ngan las 'da' ba'i rgyu'o/_/sgra ni rnam par rtog pa dang 'brel pa ste/_'khor ba 'thob par byed do/_/blo gros chen po don ni mang du thos pa rnams las rnyed par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po mang du thos pa zhes bya ba ni 'di 
[\[134a.2\]]lta ste/_don la mkhas pa yin gyi sgra la mkhas pa ma yin no/_/de la don la mkhas pa ni gang mu stegs byed smra ba thams cad dang ma 'dres par lta ba yin te/_nam bdag kyang mi ltung gzhan yang ltung bar mi byed pa de na/_blo gros chen po de ltar don la mang du thos pa don dang ldan pa yin 
[\[134a.3\]]no/_/de bas na don 'dod pas de dag la bsten par bya'o/_/de las bzlog pa ni sgra ji bzhin gyi don la mngon par chags pa yin te/_de dag ni de kho na nyid tshol bas spang bar bya'o/_/yang blo gros chen po sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis byin du brlabs pas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/
[\[134a.4\]]skye ba med pa dang /_'gog pa med pa'i lta bas ni bcom ldan 'das kyis bye brag tu bgyis pa gang yang ma mchis so/_/de ci'i slad du zhe na/_bcom ldan 'das mu stegs byed thams cad kyi rgyu rnams kyang ma skyes ma 'gags pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das khyod kyi yang nam mkha' dang ma 
[\[134a.5\]]brtags par 'gog pa dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings rnams ma skyes ma 'gags pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das mu stegs byed rnams kyang byed pa dang /_rkyen gyi rgyu las 'gro ba rnams 'byung bar brjod/_bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang rgyu de nyid la rkyen to zhes bgyi bar ming gi bye 
[\[134a.6\]]brag gzhan btags nas mi shes pa dang /_sred pa dang /_las dang /_rnam par rtog pa'i rkyen rnams las 'gro ba rnams skye bar brjod de/_khyod dang de dag phyi rol gyi rkyen rnams kyis phyi rol gyi dngos po skye ba la yang de bzhin te/_bcom ldan 'das de'i phyir smra ba 'di dang mu stegs 
[\[134a.7\]]byed kyi smra bar bye brag ma mchis par 'gyur ro/_/rdul phra mo dang /_gtso bo dang /_dbang phyug dang /_skye dgu'i bdag po la sogs pa rdzas dgu dang bcas pa rnams ma skyes ma 'gags pa ste/_bcom ldan 'das khyod kyi yang dngos po thams cad yod pa dang med pa mi dmigs pas na 
[\[134b\]]
[\[134b.1\]]ma skyes ma 'gags pa'o/_/'byung ba mi 'jig pas na rang gi mtshan nyid mi skye mi 'gag go/_/gang de dag gi rnam par gyur kyang 'byung ba rnams ni 'byung ba nyid de/_'byung ba'i rang bzhin mi 'dor ro/_/bcom ldan 'das de ni 'byung ba'i rnam par rtog pa'i 'gyur bar khyod dang mu stegs

[\[134b.3\]]na ni skye ba med pa dang /_'gog pa med pa rgyur gyur pas mu stegs byed rnams kyang sangs rgyas su 'gyur bar mchi'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jig rten gyi khams gcig tu de bzhin gshegs pa mang po 'byung ba ni gnas med de/_skabs ma yin no zhes gsungs na/_'bras bu yod 
[\[134b.4\]]pa dang med pa yongs su bzung bas bdag gi smra ba bye brag ma mchis na ni/_de bzhin gshegs pa mang por 'gyur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po nga'i skye ba med pa dang /_'gog pa med pa ni mu stegs byed rnams kyi skye ba med pa dang /_'gog pa med 
[\[134b.5\]]par smra ba dang mi mtshungs te skye ba dang /_mi rtag par smra ba dang yang mtshungs pa ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po mu stegs byed rnams kyi ni dngos po'i rang bzhin ma skyes pa/_mi 'gyur ba'i mtshan nyid thob pa yod de/_nga'i ni de ltar yod pa dang /_med pa'i phyogs su 
[\[134b.6\]]lhung ba ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po nga'i ni yod pa dang /_med pa'i phyogs dang bral ba ste/_skye ba dang 'jig pa dang bral ba dngos po yang ma yin/_dngos po med pa yang ma yin te/_sgyu ma dang rmi lam gyi gzugs sna tshogs mthong ba lta bur dngos po med pa'ang ma yin no/_/
[\[134b.7\]]ji ltar dngos po med pa ma yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_gzugs kyi rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid gzung du med pa'i phyir ro/_/snang ba dang /_mi snang ba dang /_gzung ba dang /_gzung du med pas des na de'i phyir dngos po thams cad dngos po yang ma yin/_ngo bo med pa'ang ma yin no/_/'di ltar rang 
[\[135a\]]
[\[135a.1\]]gi sems snang ba tsam khong du chud pas rnam par rtog pa mi 'jug pa'i phyir/_'jig rten rang la gnas shing bya ba med pa yin na/_byis pa rnams kyang bya ba dang ldan par rtog ste/_'phags pa rnams ni ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po de ni dri za'i grong khyer dang /_sgyu ma'i skyes

[\[135a.4\]]med do/_/sgyu ma'i skyes bu ni yod pa dang /_med pa ci yang mi byed pas na skye ba'am 'gag pa med do/_/de bzhin du chos thams cad kyang skye ba dang /_'jig pa spangs pa ste/_gzhan du na byis pa rnams log par lhung ba'i 'du shes kyis skye ba dang /_'gag par rtog pa/_'phags pa

[\[135b\]]
[\[135b.1\]]so rang gi ye shes kyis thugs su chud pa ni mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bshad do/_/de la 'di skad ces bya ste/_skye ba rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir/_/mi skye ba ni rab tu bsgrub/_/rgyu med smra ba bshad pa ni/_/byis pa rnams kyis mi rig go/_/'di dag thams cad ma skyes pa/_/dngos

[\[135b.3\]]med pa ngas bshad do/_/de bzhin 'dus pa re rer yang /_/snang ba med pas yod ma yin/_/'dus pa mu stegs lta ba ltar/_/'jig pa med par 'gyur ba min/_/rmi lam skra shad 'dzings pa dang /_/sgyu ma smig rgyu dri za'i grong /_/rgyu med pa dag snang ba ltar/_/'jig rten sna 
[\[135b.4\]]tshogs de bzhin no/_/rgyu med smra bas brtsad bya ste/_/ma skyes pa yang rab tu bsgrub/_/skye ba med par rab bsgrubs na/_/nga yi tshul ni mi 'jig go/_/rgyu med smra ba bstan na ni/_/ji ltar ci las ci dag gis/_/gang du rgyu med skyer 'gyur zhes/_/mu stegs can rnams 
[\[135b.5\]]'jigs pa skye/_/rgyu las ma yin rgyu med par/_/nam zhig 'dus byas mthong ba na/_/de na skye 'jig smra ba yi/_/lta ba rnam par ldog par 'gyur/_/dngos po med par skye med dam/_/de ste rkyen la brtag pa'am/_/'on te dngos po'i ming lags sam/_/don med pa'am 
[\[135b.6\]]bdag la gsungs/_/dngos med mi skye ma yin te/_/rkyen la brtags pa'ang ma yin no/_/dngos po'i ming yang de min te/_/don med pa yang ming ma yin/_/nyan thos dang ni rang rgyal dang /_/sa bdun du ni phyin rnams dang /_/mu stegs spyod yul min pa'o/_/de ni mi skye

[\[136a\]]
[\[136a.1\]]par ngas bshad do/_/sems ni snang las rnam grol ba/_/rang bzhin gnyis kyang spangs pa ste/_/gnas rnams rab tu gyur pa ni/_/skye ba med par ngas bshad do/_/phyi rol dngos po dngos med min/_/sems kyang yongs su gzung ba med/_/lta ba thams

[\[136b\]]
[\[136b.1\]]dag ni nang gi yin/_/'khor lo chu shing 'jim la sogs/_/sa bon 'byung sogs phyi rol lo/_/'brel pa tsam ni 'di dag min/_/gang na dngos po rkyen gzhan las/_/kha cig skye bar 'dod na ni/_/rigs dang lung la de mi gnas/_/gal te bskyed bya'i dngos med na/_/blo yang

[\[146a.1\]]la zhugs pa'i phyir yid dang /_rnam par rig pa dang bral ba'i lus de yang thob par 'gyur ro/_/yang blo gros chen pos gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das rang bzhin gsum po rnams chos lnga'i nang du 'dus sam/_'on te rang gi mtshan nyid du grub pa'am ci lags/_bcom ldan 'das 
[\[146a.2\]]kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po 'di nyid kyi nang du rang bzhin gsum dang /_rnam par shes pa brgyad dang bdag med pa gnyis kyang 'dus so/_/de la ming dang mtshan ma ni yongs su kun brtags pa'i rang bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/gang yang blo gros chen po de la gnas shing zhugs pa'i rnam par rtog 
[\[146a.3\]]pa sems dang sems las byung ba zhes bsgrags pa nyi ma dang 'od zer lhan cig pa bzhin dus gcig tu byung ba sna tshogs kyi mtshan nyid rang gi rnam par rtog pa khong du chud par byed pa'i rang bzhin ni blo gros chen po gzhan gyi dbang zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po yang dag pa'i ye shes dang de bzhin nyid ni mi 'jig 
[\[146a.4\]]pa'i phyir rang bzhin yongs su grub pa shes par bya'o/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po rang gi sems snang ba la mngon par chags pas ni rnam par rtog pa brgyad du phye ste/_mtshan ma yang dag pa ma yin pa'i mtshan nyid la kun brtags pa'i phyir/_bdag dang bdag gir 'dzin pa gnyis rnam par zhi bas bdag 
[\[146a.5\]]med pa gnyis skye'o/_/blo gros chen po chos lnga po 'di rnams kyi nang du sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang sa rnam par dbye ba'i mtshams sbyar ba dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi bdag gi 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i ye shes rtogs 
[\[146a.6\]]pa la 'jug pa rnams kyang 'dus so/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po chos lnga rnams ni mtshan ma dang /_ming dang /_rnam par rtog pa dang /_de bzhin nyid dang yang dag pa'i ye shes te/_blo gros chen po de la mtshan ma ni gang /_dbyibs dang /_rnam pa dang /_khyad par dang /_byad dang gzugs la 
[\[146a.7\]]sogs pa'i mtshan nyid du snang ba de ni mtshan ma'o/_/gang mtshan ma de nyid la rdza ma la sogs par ming btags te/_'di ni de bzhin gyi gzhan du ma yin no zhes bya ba de ni ming ngo /_/blo gros chen po gang gis bde ba zhe'am/_go cha zhes ming de brjod cing mtshan ma gsal bar byed pa 
[\[146b\]]
[\[146b.1\]]sems dang sems las byung bar bsgrags pa de ni rnam par rtog pa'o/_/chos de dag phan tshun du myong ba med cing kun brtags pa'ang ma yin pa'i phyir/_gang blo rab tu zhig gi bar du ming dang /_mtshan ma shin tu mi dmigs pa ni de bzhin nyid de kho na nyid yang dag par nges pa 
[\[146b.2\]]mthar thug pa/_rang bzhin dang /_ngo bo nyid dang /_mi dmigs pa de bzhin nyid kyi mtshan nyid du nga dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa gzhan dag gis kyang thugs su chud de/_ji lta ba bzhin du bshad do/_/btags so/_/bkrol lo/_/gsal bar byas so/_/gang gis 
[\[146b.3\]]khongs su chud cing yang dag par rtogs pa dang /_gang chad pa med pa dang /_rtag pa med pa'i phyir rnam par rtog par mi 'jug pa/_bdag gi 'phags pa so so rang gi ye shes dang 'thun pa/_pha rol gyi phyogs su mu stegs byed dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas 
[\[146b.4\]]rnams kyi spyod yul ma yin pa de ni yang dag pa'i ye shes te/_blo gros chen po de dag ni chos rnam pa lnga'o/_/'di dag nyid kyi nang du rang bzhin gsum dang /_rnam par shes pa brgyad dang /_bdag med pa gnyis dang /_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyang 'dus te/_
[\[146b.5\]]blo gros chen po de la khyod kyis rang gi blo la mkhas par bya'o/_/gzhan yang byed du gzhug cing gzhan gyi dring la gzhog par mi bya'o/_/de la 'di skad ces bya ste/_chos lnga dag dang rang bzhin dang /_/rnam shes brgyad po nyid dag dang /_/bdag med rnam pa gnyis par 
[\[146b.6\]]ni/_/theg chen thams cad 'dus pa yin/_/ming dang mtshan ma kun brtags pa/_/rang bzhin gnyis kyi mtshan nyid de/_/yang dag ye shes de bzhin nyid/_/yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
[\[146b.7\]]chen po blo gros chen pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/gang 'di skad du bcom ldan 'das kyis bstan pa brjod pa las de bzhin gshegs pa 'das pa dang /_ma byon pa dang /_da ltar gyi gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed do zhes gsung na/_bcom ldan 'das 'di la sgra ji bzhin gyi don 
[\[147a\]]
[\[147a.1\]]gzung bar bgyi'am/_'on te don gyi bye brag gzhan zhig mchis sam ci lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis de bka' stsal du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po sgra ji bzhin gyi don du gzung bar mi bya ste/_blo gros chen po gang 
[\[147a.2\]]gA'i klung gi bye ma'i tshad kyis ni dus gsum gcig gi sangs rgyas kyi tshad du'ang mi 'gyur te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_'jig rten gyi phul las 'das pa dang 'dra ba dag dang /_mi 'dra ba'i phyir dpe ni dpe ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 
[\[147a.3\]]bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni 'jig rten gyi phul las 'das pa dang 'dra bas mi 'dra ba'i dpe mi ston te/_blo gros chen po gzhan du na rtag pa dang /_mi rtag pa la mngon par zhen pa'i byis pa so so'i skye bo mu stegs byed kyi lta ba ngan pas brlag pa/_'khor ba 
[\[147a.4\]]srid pa'i 'khor lo'i rjes su 'brang ba'i rnams skyo bar bya ba'i phyir ji ltar 'di dag srid pa'i 'gro ba srid pa'i 'khor lo nyam nga ba de las skyo bar gyur te/_khyad par 'dod pas khyad par du rtsom par 'gyur zhing /_sangs rgyas thob pa sla bar bstan pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[147a.5\]]rnams skye ba ni u dum ba ra'i me tog lta bu yin no zhes brtson 'grus rtsom par 'gyur bas/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed ces bya ba 'di ni dpe tsam du bzhag pa'o/_/bstan pa brjod pa las ngas 'dul 
[\[147a.6\]]ba'i skye bo rnams bltas nas de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'byung ba ni u dum bA ra'i me tog bzhin du rnyed par dka'o zhes bshad mod kyi/_blo gros chen po u dum bA ra'i me tog ni sngon sus kyang ma mthong zhing mthong bar mi 'gyur na/_blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[147a.7\]]rnams ni 'jig rten du mthong zhing da ltar yang snang ste/_rang gi tshul so sor gzhag pa'i gtam gyi dbang du bya bar de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'byung ba ni u dum bA ra'i me tog bzhin rnyed par dka'o zhes ni ma bshad do/_/blo gros chen po rang gi tshul so sor gzhag pa'i 
[\[147b\]]
[\[147b.1\]]gtam bshad na 'jig rten gyi phul las 'das pa'i dpe rigs pa rnams mi bya ste/_yid ches par mi rung bas na byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams dad par mi 'gyur ro/_/de kho na nyid ni sems dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa las 'das pa'i phyir bdag gi 'phags pa so so rang 
[\[147b.2\]]gi ye shes kyi spyod yul la ni dpe rnams mi 'jug go/_/de kho na nyid ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams yin te/_de'i phyir na de dag la dpe rnams gzhag par mi bya mod kyi/_blo gros chen po 'on kyang 'di ni dpe tsam du byas pa 'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ni gang gA'i klung 
[\[147b.3\]]gi bye ma snyed dang mnyam ste/_mi rtog rnam par mi rtog pas na mnyam zhing mi mnyam pa med do/_/blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/_gang gA'i klung gi bye ma rnams ni nya dang /_ru sbal dang /_chu srin byis pa gsod dang /_mi dang /_ma he dang /_seng ge dang /_glang po dang /_rta la 
[\[147b.4\]]sogs pas dkrugs kyang dkrugs so zhe'am/_ma dkrugs so zhes mi rtog rnam par mi rtog cing dang sa la dri ma dang bral te rnam par rtog pa med do/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi bdag gi 'phags 
[\[147b.5\]]pa so so rang gi ye shes ni gang gA'i klung gi stobs dang /_dbang dang /_mngon par shes pa'i bye ma rnams byis pa mu stegs byed pha rol rgol ba'i nya thams cad kyis dkrugs kyang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis sngon gyi smon lam btab pa'i phyir mi rtog rnam par mi rtog ste/_bde 
[\[147b.6\]]ba'i snyoms par 'jug pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs pas sems can rnams la mi rtog rnam par mi rtog go/_/de bas na de bzhin gshegs pa de dag ni mdza' ba dang /_khong khro ba med pa'i phyir gang gA'i klung gi bye ma dang mnyam ste/_bye brag med do/_/'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po 
[\[147b.7\]]gang gA'i klung gi bye ma rnams ni sa'i mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin yin pas bskal pa 'tshig pa nas ni bsregs kyang sa'i rang bzhin mi 'dor ro/_/blo gros chen po me'i khams dang 'brel pa'i phyir sa ni mi 'tshig ste/_gzhan du na byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams log par ltung ba'i blos 
[\[148a\]]
[\[148a.1\]]'tshig par rtog mod kyi/_de ni me'i rgyu las byung bas na mi 'tshig go/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi chos kyi sku yang gang gA'i klung gi bye ma dang mnyam ste mi 'jig go/_/'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po gang gA'i klung gi bye 
[\[148a.2\]]ma rnams ni tshad med de/_de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi 'od zer snang ba tshad med pa yang sems can yongs su smin par bskul ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du gtong ngo /_/'di 
[\[148a.3\]]lta ste/_blo gros chen po gang gA'i klung gi bye ma rnams rang bzhin gzhan du mi rtsom ste/_bye ma rnams ni bye ma'i gnas nyid do/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni srid pa thams cad du 'jug pa'i 
[\[148a.4\]]rgyu chad pa yin pas na 'khor bar 'jug pa yang med/_ldog pa yang med do/_/'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po gang gA'i klung gi bye ma rnams ni phri yang mngon pa med de/_bsnan kyang mngon pa med do/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po chos la lus med pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs 
[\[148a.5\]]pa'i ye shes kyang sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i sbyor bas mi zad mi 'phel lo/_/blo gros chen po chos ni lus med de lus dang ldan pa la ni 'jig pa yod kyi lus dang mi ldan pa la ni ma yin no/_/'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po gang gA'i klung gi bye ma rnams ni mar dang 'bru mar 'dod pas 
[\[148a.6\]]rab tu btsir kyang mar dang 'bru mar med pa de bzhin du/_blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ni sems can gyi sdug bsngal dag gis rab tu gzir kyang snying rje chen po dang ldan pa'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis sems can thams cad mya ngan las ma bzlas kyi bar du chos kyi 
[\[148a.7\]]dbyings kyi rang gi ngang gi smon lam gyi bde ba mi 'dor ro/_/'di lta ste/_blo gros chen po gang gA'i klung gi bye ma rnams ni 'bab pa'i rgyun du 'thun par 'bab ste/_chu med par ni ma yin no/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi chos bstan pa thams cad kyang mya ngan las 
[\[148b\]]
[\[148b.1\]]'das pa 'bab pa'i rgyun du 'thun par 'jug ste/_de'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa rnams gang gA'i klung gi bye ma dang mnyam zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po 'gro ba'i don 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la mi 'jug go/_/blo gros chen po 'jug pa ni 'gro ba'i don yin te/_blo gros 
[\[148b.2\]]chen po 'khor ba'i thog ma'i mtha' shes pa yang med de/_mi shes na ji ltar 'gro ba'i don du bshad/_blo gros chen po 'gro ba'i don ni chad pa yin na/_byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams kyis ni rab tu mi shes so/_/blo gros chen pos gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de gal te/_
[\[148b.3\]]sems can rnams 'khor ba'i thog ma'i mtha' shes pa yang med na/_de ji ltar srog chags rnams thar par shes/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po rang gi sems snang ba phyi rol gyi don yongs su shes pas thog ma med pa'i dus kyi spros pa/_gnas 
[\[148b.4\]]ngan len gyi rnam par rtog pa'i bag chags kyi rgyu rnam par log cing rang gi gnas shin tu gyur pa ni thar pa yin te/_blo gros chen po 'jig pa ni ma yin no/_/de bas na blo gros chen po mtha' med pa'i gtam ni cir yang dgos pa ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po mtha' med pa'i mtha' zhes 
[\[148b.5\]]bya ba 'di ni rnam par rtog pa'i rnam grangs te/_'di la blos yongs su brtags na rnam par rtog pa las phyi'am nang gi sems can gzhan gang yang med do/_/blo gros chen po chos thams cad ni shes pa dang shes par bya bas dben pa yin te/_gzhan du na rang gi sems snang ba'i rnam par 
[\[148b.6\]]rtog pa yongs su mi shes pa'i phyir rnam par rtog pa 'jug ste/_de khong du chud pas ldog par 'gyur ro/_/de la 'di skad ces bya ste/_gang gis gang gA'i bye mnyam du/_/'jig med tshul gyi mthar thug pa/_/'dren pa rab tu mthong ba ni/_/de dag 'dren pa mthong ba'o/_/gang gA'i 
[\[148b.7\]]bye ma ji bzhin du/_/nyes pa thams cad rnam spangs te/_/'bab phyogs 'thun zhing rtag pa ltar/_/de bzhin sangs rgyas sangs rgyas nyid/_/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das

[\[149a.1\]]bde bar gshegs pas chos thams cad skad cig tu 'gyur bas 'jig pa bdag la bshad du gsol/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis bdag la bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na chos thams cad 
[\[149a.2\]]skad cig pa lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang ngas khyod la bshad do/_/legs so bcom ldan 'das zhes gsol nas/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po 
[\[149a.3\]]bcom ldan 'das la slar nyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo gros chen po chos thams cad chos thams cad ces bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_dge ba dang /_mi dge ba dang /_'dus byas dang /_'dus ma byas dang /_'jig rten pa dang /_
[\[149a.4\]]'jig rten las 'das pa dang /_kha na ma tho ba dang bcas pa dang /_kha na ma tho ba med pa dang /_zag pa dang bcas pa dang /_zag pa med pa dang /_zin pa dang /_ma zin pa rnams yin te blo gros chen po mdor na nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga sems dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par 
[\[149a.5\]]shes pa'i bag chags kyi rgyu las byung ba rnams ni sems dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i bag chags rgyas pa'i byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams kyis dge ba dang /_mi dge bar yongs su brtags so/_/blo gros chen po ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba dang /_snyoms par 'jug 
[\[149a.6\]]pa rnams ni tshe 'di la bde bar gnas pa'i dngos pos 'phags pa rnams kyi dge ba zag pa med pa zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po yang dge ba dang mi dge ba ni 'di lta ste/_rnam par shes pa brgyad rnams so/_/brgyad gang zhe na 'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po kun gzhi rnam par 
[\[149a.7\]]shes pa zhes bsgrags pa dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang /_mu stegs byed kyis brjod pa'i rnam par shes pa 'dus pa lnga rnams so/_/blo gros chen po de la rnam par shes pa 'dus pa lnga rnams yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang bcas pa dge ba dang /_mi dge ba'i skad cig ma

[\[149b.1\]]gcig nas gcig tu dbye bas tha dad du gyur pa/_rgyud kyi rgyun gyis lus tha mi dad pa 'byung ngo /_/byung nas kyang 'jig par 'gyur ro/_/rang gi sems snang ba ma rtogs pa'i phyir 'gags ma thag tu rnam par shes pa gzhan 'byung ste/_dbyibs byad kyi bye brag 'dzin pa'i yid kyi rnam par shes pa

[\[149b.5\]]po de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po ni bde ba dang sdug bsngal mi myong ba'i rgyu dang bcas pa bag chags bzhi dang ldan pas 'jug cing ldog ste/_byis pa skad cig mar lta ba'i rnam par rtog pas bsgos pa'i blo can dag gis ni mi rtogs so/_/blo gros chen po gzhan yang gser dang /_

[\[150a.1\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad du pha rol tu phyin pa drug yongs su rdzogs par byas na sangs rgyas thob par 'gyur ro zhes bka' stsal to/_/pha rol tu phyin pa de dag gang lags/_ji ltar na yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal 
[\[150a.2\]]pa/_blo gros chen po pha rol tu phyin pa'i bye brag ni 'di gsum ste/_gsum gang zhe na 'di lta ste/_'jig rten pa dang 'jig rten las 'das pa dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa'i mchog go/_/blo gros chen po de la 'jig rten pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i bdag dang /_bdag gir 'dzin pa la

[\[150a.4\]]'grus dang /_bsam gtan dang /_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par byed de/_tshangs par skye bar bya ba'i phyir mngon par shes pa dag kyang mngon par sgrub bo/_/de la 'jig rten las 'das pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa rnams kyis ni byis pa rnams bdag bde ba mya ngan las

[\[150a.6\]]'byor dam pa la rnal 'byor pa rnams rang gi sems snang ba tsam du 'dzin pa dang /_rang gi sems gnyis su rtogs pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa mi 'jug pa dang /_len pa la 'dzin pa med pa dang /_rang gi sems dang /_gzugs kyi mtshan nyid la mngon par ma chags pas sems 
[\[150a.7\]]can thams cad la phan pa dang /_bde ba'i phyir sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/gang dmigs pa de nyid la rnam par rtog par mi 'jug par sgom pa de ni tshul khrims te/_pha rol tu phyin pa'ang de'o/_/gang gzung ba dang 'dzin pa yongs su shes pas rnam par rtog pa de nyid

[\[150b.1\]]mi 'jug par bzod pa de ni bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o/_/brtson 'grus gang gis nam gyi cha stod dang /_nam gyi cha smad la brtson par byed cing rnal 'byor dang 'thun par mthong bas rnam par rtog pa ldog pa'i phyir de ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o/_/gang rnam par rtog pa bzlog pa'i

[\[160a.2\]]sna tshogs mi 'byung ngo /_/stong pa ngo bo nyid med pa/_/sgyu ma bzhin du ma skyes rnams/_/yod dang med pa ma yin te/_/dngos po 'di dag rmi lam bzhin/_/nga ni rang bzhin gcig 'chad pa/_/rtog ge mtshan ma rigs spangs pa/_/'phags pa'i lha yi spyod yul te/_/
[\[160a.3\]]rang bzhin rnam gnyis spangs pa'o/_/'dzin pa med cing gzung ba med/_/thar pa med cing 'ching ba med/_/sgyu ma dang ni smig rgyu 'dra/_/rmi lam rab rib ji bzhin du/_/gang tshe yang dag don 'dod pa/_/rnam par mi rtog chags med pas/_/mthong ba de la rnal 'byor zhugs/_/
[\[160a.4\]]the tsom med par nga yang mthong /_/de la rnam rig gang yang med/_/ji ltar nam mkha'i smig rgyu bzhin/_/chos rnams de ltar shes na ni/_/ci yang shes pa med pa'o/_/khams rnams rab tu 'khrugs pa'i phyir/_/srin bu me khyer lta bu dag_/med kyang myos la sna tshogs snang /_/
[\[160a.5\]]de bzhin 'jig rten rang bzhin ni/_/rtswa dang shing dang gyo mo las/_/sgyu ma rnam par mdzes gyur kyang /_/sgyu ma de ni med pa ste/_/chos kyi rang bzhin de bzhin no/_/rkyen rnams yod dang med pa las/_/chos rnams 'byung ba med pa ste/_/khams gsum na ni nor ba yi/_/de phyir sna 
[\[160a.6\]]tshogs snang bar 'gyur/_/'jig rten rmi lam ngo bo nyid mtshungs te/_/de na sna tshogs kyi yang gzugs rnams snang /_/reg dang longs spyod lus kyi mthar 'gro dang /_/'jig rten gtso dang byed pa rnams mtshungs so/_/sems 'di khams gsum skye gnas gyur pa yin/_/sems 
[\[160a.7\]]'khrul 'di dang pha rol snang ba'o/_/'jig rten med phyir de la brtag mi bya/_/'jig rten de 'dra'i rang bzhin rtogs nas ni/_/mi gtsang gnas kyi tha mar phyin par 'gyur/_/byis pa rnams ni gti mug phyir/_/'byung ba dang ni 'jig pa mthong /_/shes rab ldan pa rnams dag ni/_/
[\[160b\]]
[\[160b.1\]]'byung ba 'jig pa mthong ba med/_/lha yi pho brang 'og min no/_/sdig pa thams cad rnam spangs par/_/rtag tu rnam par mi rtog ldan/_/sems dang sems las byung ba spangs/_/stobs dang mngon shes dbang thob pa/_/de dag ting 'dzin dbang rnyed nas/_/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
[\[160b.2\]]der 'tshang rgya/_/sprul pa rnams ni 'dir 'tshang rgya/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi sprul pa dag_/bye ba dpag med 'byung bar 'gyur/_/byis pa kun ni de dag las/_/brag ca 'dra chos bsam yas nyon/_/thog ma dbus dang mtha' las grol/_/dngos po dang ni dngos med spangs/_/khyab pa 
[\[160b.3\]]mi g.yo dag pa ste/_/sna tshogs med la sna tshogs 'byung /_/rnam par rig pa'i rigs kyis g.yogs/_/lus can kun la gnas pa ste/_/sgyu ma nor ba yod mod kyi/_/sgyu ma nor ba'i rgyu ma yin/_/'jig rten dag na sems slu ba/_/cung zad tsam ni gang yod pa/_/rang bzhin gnyis 
[\[160b.4\]]dang 'brel ba ste/_/kun gzhi rnam par shes pas sprul/_/rnam rig tsam gyi lta shwa dang /_/gang zag chos su spyod par yang /_/'jig rten de ltar rnam bsgoms nas/_/nam zhig shin tu gyur pa na/_/de tshe nga yi bu yin te/_/rdzogs pa'i chos rnams rab ston pa/_/drod dang 
[\[160b.5\]]gsher dang sra g.yo bar/_/chos rnams byis pas rnam par brtags/_/yang dag ma yin sgro btags te/_/mtshan nyid med cing mtshan gzhi med/_/rgyun rnams kyi ni lus 'di la/_/'di ni rdzas brgyad gzugs yin te/_/lus kyi dbyibs dang dbang por ni/_/byis pa nor ba 
[\[160b.6\]]rnams rtog go/_/rgyu dang rkyen rnams tshogs pa las/_/byis pa 'byung bar rtog par byed/_/tshul 'di dag ni mi shes pas/_/srid pa gsum gyi gnas na 'khyam/_/dngos po thams cad rang bzhin med pa ste/_/rtog pa me yi tshig yin de yang med/_/srid pa rmi lam sprul bzhin 
[\[160b.7\]]brtags byas nas/_/'khor bar mi bya mya ngan 'das mi bya/_/sna tshogs sa bon zhes bya'i sems/_/sems kyi spyod yul la snang ste/_/snang nas rtog pa gnyis dag gi/_/byis pa rnams ni skye bar rtog_/mi shes pa dang sred dang las/_/sems dang sems las byung gnas te/_/
[\[161a\]]
[\[161a.1\]]gang phyir de las 'byung bas na/_/de ni gzhan gyi dbang du 'dod/_/de dag gang dngos rtog pa ni/_/sems kyi spyod yul nor ba ste/_/rtog pas grub pa ma yin no/_/log par nor ba rnam par brtags/_/rkyen rnams dang ni 'brel pa las/_/lus can rnams 
[\[161a.2\]]kyi sems 'byung ngo /_/rkyen rnams las ni rnam grol zhing /_/rang gi mtshan nyid rnam spangs te/_/nam zhig lus la mi gnas na/_/de phyir nga yi spyod yul min/_/ji ltar rgyal po tshong dpon dag_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]'dra ba sna tshogs kyis/_/bu brid khyim na rtser bcug nas/_/
[\[161a.3\]]nags kyi [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]phrad pa bstan/_/de ltar ngas ni chos rnams kyi/_/gzugs brnyan tsam nyid sna tshogs kyis/_/so so rang rig yang dag mtha'/_/bu rnams la ni nga 'chad do/_/ji ltar rgya mtsho'i rlabs rnams ni/_/rlung gi rkyen gyis byung ba dag_/gar byed rab tu 'jug par 
[\[161a.4\]]'gyur/_/rgyun kyang chad par mi 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin kun gzhi chu bo yang /_/rtag tu yul gyi rkyen gyis byung /_/rnam shes dba' rlabs sna tshogs kyis/_/gar byed rab tu 'dud par 'gyur/_/gzung dang 'dzin pa'i dngos por ni/_/lus can rnams kyi sems 'jug go/_/
[\[161a.5\]]ji ltar byis pas brtags pa ltar/_/snang ba yi ni mtshan nyid med/_/kun gzhi rnam shes dam pa ni/_/rnam par rig ste kun gzhi yang /_/gzung dang 'dzin pa spangs pa'i phyir/_/de bzhin nyid du ngas bshad do/_/phung po rnams la bdag med de/_/srog kyang med cing 
[\[161a.6\]]gang zag med/_/rnam par shes pa skye bar 'gyur/_/rnam par shes ni 'gags par 'gyur/_/ji ltar ri mo mthon dman du/_/snang yang yod pa ma yin pa/_/de bzhin dngos po rnams la yang /_/dngos nyid snang yang yod pa min/_/ji ltar dri za'i grong khyer dang /_/
[\[161a.7\]]ji lta bur ni smig rgyu dag_/de ltar snang ba rtag mthong yang /_/shes rab kyis ni yod ma yin/_/tshad ma dbang po rnam par log_/'bras bu med cing rgyu yang med/_/blo dang rig par bya dang bral/_/mtshan dang mtshan gyi gzhi yang spangs/_/phung por rten cing sangs rgyas pa/_/
[\[161b\]]
[\[161b.1\]]gang na'ang sus kyang ma mthong ste/_/gang na sus gang ma mthong ba/_/de la ji ltar rnam par bsgom/_/rkyen dang gtan tshigs dpe rnams dang /_/dam 'cha' ba dang rgyu rnams kyis/_/rmi lam dri za'i 'khor lo dang /_/smig rgyu nyi ma zla ba dang /_/[{]myi [,]mi [}]gu 'od sogs dpe rnams kyis/_/
[\[161b.2\]]skye ba de dag ngas bzlog go/_/'gro ba rmi lam sgyu ma 'dra/_/stong pa dang ni brtags pa ste/_/'jig rten gsum la mi gnas shing /_/phyi nang du yang de bzhin te/_/srid pa ma skyes mthong bas na/_/mi skye bzod pa 'byung bar 'gyur/_/sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin/_/lus kyang sgyu 
[\[161b.3\]]ma lta bu ste/_/mngon shes dbang dang stobs rnams kyang /_/de yi sems la sna tshogs 'gyur/_/gang gi dngos po ma skyes pa/_/stong pa shin tu rang bzhin med/_/de dag la ni rkyen rnams kyis/_/nor ba skye zhing 'gag par 'gyur/_/phyi rol snang ba'i gzugs can gyis/_/sems 
[\[161b.4\]]dang sems ni snang bar 'gyur/_/ji ltar byis pas brtags pa ltar/_/mthong bar bya ba gzhan med do/_/'brel ba rnams dang sangs rgyas gzugs/_/'byung ba rnams ni 'jig pa dang /_/shin tu bslabs pas rnam rig gis/_/'gro ba sna tshogs byin gyis rlob/_/lus dang gnas 
[\[161b.5\]]dang longs spyod rnams/_/gzung ba rnam rig gsum po ste/_/yid dang 'dzin pa'i rnam rig dang /_/rnam par rtog ni 'dzin pa gsum/_/rnam par rtog dang rnam brtag pa/_/ji srid yi ge'i spyod yul ba/_/rtog can rtog ges nor pa'i phyir/_/de srid yang dag mi mthong ngo /_/gang 
[\[161b.6\]]gi tshe na shes rab kyis/_/dngos po rang bzhin med par shes/_/de yi tshe na rnal 'byor can/_/mtshan med gnas shing ngal yang bso/_/bya gag snag tsas bskus pa la/_/de nyid thi bar mi shes 'dzin/_/mi shes pa yi byis pa dag_/theg pa gsum la'ang de bzhin no/_/'di la 
[\[161b.7\]]nyan thos gang yang med/_/rang rgyal ba dag de bzhin te/_/nyan thos dang ni rang rgyal ba'i/_/gang gzugs 'di dag snang ba ni/_/byang chub sems dpa' snying brtse can/_/de dag sprul pa ston pa'o/_/srid pa gsum ni rnam rig tsam/_/rang bzhin gnyis ni brtags pa ste/_/chos 
[\[162a\]]
[\[162a.1\]]dang gang zag 'phos pa'i phyir/_/de gyur pa ni de bzhin nyid/_/nyi ma zla ba mar me'i 'od/_/de bzhin 'byung ba nor bu rnams/_/rnam par mi rtog shin tu 'byung /_/de bzhin sangs rgyas sangs rgyas nyid/_/ji ltar rab rib can dag gis/_/skra shad log par 'dzings 
[\[162a.2\]]pa ltar/_/de bzhin dngos por rtog pa 'di/_/byis pas log pa rnam par brtags/_/nyon mongs rnam byang dngos po rnams/_/skra shad 'dzings pa lta bu ste/_/gnas dang 'jig dang skye dang bral/_/rtag dang mi rtag spangs pa'o/_/ji ltar la las d+ha tur zos/_/gser lta 
[\[162a.3\]]bur ni 'gro ba mthong /_/de na gser yod ma yin yang /_/sa dag kyang ni gser du snang /_/thog ma med sems sems byung bas/_/de ltar byis pa nyams par byas/_/srid pa sgyu ma smig rgyu skyes/_/yang dag nyid du 'dzin par byed/_/sa bon gcig dang sa bon med/_/mnyam 
[\[162a.4\]]dang shas che sa bon can/_/sa bon kun kyang de bzhin no/_/sna tshogs gyur gyi sems la ltos/_/nam dag pa na sa bon gcig_/shin tu gyur pa sa bon med/_/mi rtog pas na mnyam pa'o/_/shas che bas na tshe rabs 'dres/_/sa bon sna tshogs 'debs pas na/_/de ni 
[\[162a.5\]]sa bon kun ces bya/_/'di la rkyen gyis mi skye ste/_/gang yang 'gag par mi 'gyur ro/_/brtags pa yi ni rkyen rnams nyid/_/skye ba dang ni 'gag par 'gyur/_/srid pa gsum ni btags tsam ste/_/ngo bo nyid kyis dngos po med/_/btags pa dngos po'i ngo bor ni/_/rtog ge 
[\[162a.6\]]pa dag rtog par 'gyur/_/dngos po'i rang bzhin shes 'dod la/_/nor pa dag ni mi rtogs so/_/dngos po'i rang bzhin mi skye ba/_/de ltar mthong nas rnam par 'grol/_/sgyu ma med dang chos mtshungs pas/_/dngos po rnams ni yod brjod bzhin/_/log pa glog ltar mgyogs 
[\[162a.7\]]pas na/_/de phyir sgyu ma lta bur rig_/bskyed pa med cing skyes pa med/_/rkyen rnams kyang ni gang na yang /_/gang yang yod pa ma yin te/_/kha cig gis ni spyod par brjod/_/rkyen gyi skye 'jig 'brel ba ni/_/bzlog par bya ba ma yin te/_/rang bzhin med cing rnam rig med/_/rkyen rnams 
[\[162b\]]
[\[162b.1\]]kyis ni byis pa dag_/gang la rtog pa de bzlog go/_/rang bzhin med cing rnam rig med/_/kun gzhi med cing dngos med na/_/byis pa ngan pa'i rtog ge pa/_/ro dang 'dra bas 'di dag brtags/_/nam zhig 'jig rten sems tsam du/_/rgyal ba'i sras kyis mthong ba na/_/
[\[162b.2\]]de tshe ris su gtogs pa'i lus/_/byis pa'i 'du byed spangs pa ste/_/stobs dang mngon par shes pa dang /_/dbang dang ldan pa rab tu 'thob/_/thams cad gzugs su snang ba yi/_/sems de nam zhig byung ba na/_/'di la sems med gzugs med kyi/_/nor sems 
[\[162b.3\]]thog ma med ces pa/_/de tshe rnal 'byor can gyi rnams/_/shes pas 'gro ba snang med mthong /_/mtshan ma dngos po rnam rig dang /_/gang rig rnam par g.yos pa las/_/nga yi bu rnams 'das nas ni/_/de dag rnam par mi rtog spyod/_/sgyu ma dri za'i grong khyer dang /_/skra 
[\[162b.4\]]shad 'dzings pa'i smig rgyu dag_/med bzhin du ni yod par snang /_/de bzhin dngos po dngos nyid do/_/dngos po thams cad ma skyes te/_/nor ba tsam ni snang bar 'gyur/_/byis pa rtog gnyis dga' ba dang /_/nor pa las ni skyes par rtog_/sna tshogs bag chags las byung ba'i/_/
[\[162b.5\]]skye ba'i char ni gtogs pa'i sems/_/chu bo'i rlabs bzhin shin tu 'byung /_/de chad nas ni mi 'byung ngo /_/rnam pa sna tshogs dmigs pa'i sems/_/sna tshogs la ni 'jug pa bzhin/_/de bzhin nam mkha' rtsig pa la/_/ci yi phyir na 'jug mi 'gyur/_/mtshan ma zhig la dmigs nas ni/_/
[\[162b.6\]]gal te sems ni 'jug 'gyur na/_/sems kyang rkyen gyis bskyed 'gyur te/_/sems tsam du ni mi rung ngo /_/sems kyis su ni sems la gzung /_/rgyu dang bcas pa gang yang med/_/sems kyi chos nyid dag pa ste/_/nam mkha' la ni bag chags med/_/rang gi sems la mngon chags 
[\[162b.7\]]pas/_/sems ni shin tu 'byung bar 'gyur/_/phyi rol snang ba yod med de/_/de yi phyir na sems tsam mo/_/sems ni kun gzhi rnam shes te/_/ngar sems nyid ni yid yin no/_/gang gis yul rnams 'dzin pa ni/_/de ni rnam par shes zhes bya/_/sems ni rtag tu lung ma bstan/_/
[\[163a\]]
[\[163a.1\]]yid ni gnyi gar rgyu ba ste/_/rnam par shes pa 'byung ba yang /_/de ni dge dang mi dge'o/_/lus dang gnas dang longs spyod ni/_/gzung ba'i rnam rig gsum pa'o/_/yid dang 'dzin pa'i rnam rig dang /_/rnam par rtog pa 'dzin pa gsum/_/rnam rig gnyis po spangs 
[\[163a.2\]]pa ni/_/dam pa'i don gyi sgo yin te/_/snang ba med la 'dug na ni/_/theg pa gsum gzhag ga la yod/_/sems tsam dang ni snang med dang /_/sangs rgyas sa dang gnas rnams ni/_/de dag sangs rgyas rnams kyis bshad/_/'chad cing 'chang bar 'gyur ba'o/_/sa bdun dag ni 
[\[163a.3\]]sems yin te/_/snang ba med pa brgyad pa'o/_/'dir ni sa gnyis gnas yin te/_/sa gzhan nga yi rang bzhin no/_/ngag dang so so rang rig pa/_/sa 'di nga yi rang bzhin te/_/dbang phyug chen po'i gnas kyi mchog_/'og min shin tu rnam par mdzes/_/me yi ji lta de bzhin 
[\[163a.4\]]du/_/de yi 'od zer sna tshogs pa/_/yid 'ong zhi ba rab tu 'byung /_/de dag srid gsum sprul par byed/_/srid pa gsum po kha cig sprul/_/kha cig snga nas sprul pa ste/_/der ni theg pa rnams ston pa/_/de ni nga yi rang bzhin sa/_/zhing du 'pho dang rtogs pa la/_/sa rnams kyi 
[\[163a.5\]]ni dus med de/_/sems tsam las kyang 'das nas ni/_/snang med gnas pa'i 'bras bu'o/_/rnam pa sna tshogs yod pa dang /_/med pa dag kyang snang bar 'gyur/_/byis pa 'dzin pas rnam bslad de/_/sna tshogs pa ni phyin ci log_/nam zhig mi rtog ye shes ni/_/dngos po yod 
[\[163a.6\]]par mi rung ngo /_/gang phyir sems ni gzugs min pas/_/de phyir de ni rnam mi rtog_/dbang po rnams ni sgyu ma bzhin/_/yul rnams kyang ni rmi lam 'dra/_/byed dang las dang bya ba 'di/_/rnam pa kun tu yod pa min/_/bsam gtan rnams dang tshad med dang /_/gzugs med pa dang ting 
[\[163a.7\]]'dzin dang /_/'du shes 'gog pa ma lus par/_/sems tsam la ni yod pa min/_/rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu dang /_/lan cig phyir 'ong 'bras bu dang /_/phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /_/dgra bcom nyid ni sems nor pa'o/_/byis pa rnams ni 'dus byas la/_/mi rtag skad cig 
[\[163b\]]
[\[163b.1\]]stong par rtog_/sa bon 'bab chu mar me dpes/_/skad cig pa yi don du rtog_/byed pa med cing skad cig pa/_/dben par bya ba spangs pa dang /_/chos rnams kyis ni mi skye ba/_/skad cig don du ngas bshad do/_/yod dang med las skyes par ni/_/khyad par pa dang grangs can shes/_/
[\[163b.2\]]lung du ma bstan thams cad kyang /_/de dag nyid kyis bshad pa'o/_/lung bstan pa ni rnam bzhi ste/_/mgo gcig dang ni dri ba dang /_/rnam par dbye dang gzhag pa rnams/_/mu stegs smra ba bzlog pa'o/_/kun rdzob tu ni thams cad yod/_/dam pa'i don du yod ma yin/_/chos rnams 
[\[163b.3\]]rang bzhin med pa la/_/dam pa'i don du 'dod na ni/_/dmigs pa rang bzhin med pa la/_/de phyir kun rdzob bstan pa yin/_/dngos po brjod pa'i rgyus byung ba/_/gal te rang bzhin yod na ni/_/brjod pa dag las byung ba yi/_/dngos po med ces bya ba med/_/brjod pa 'di na dngos med de/_/
[\[163b.4\]]kun rdzob tu yang yod ma yin/_/phyin ci log ni yod min pas/_/dmigs pa yod pa ma yin no/_/gal te phyin ci log yod na/_/ngo bo nyid med med par 'gyur/_/phyin ci log ni bzhi yi phyir/_/gang dang gang dag dmigs gyur pa/_/de ni rang bzhin med par 'gyur/_/thams cad du yang med pa 
[\[163b.5\]]yin/_/gang 'di sna tshogs snang ba ni/_/gnas ngan len gyis bsgos pa'i sems/_/gzugs su snang ba 'dzin pa ste/_/phyi rol dag ni sems 'khrul ba/_/rtog pa dang ni mi rtog pas/_/rnam rtog rab tu spong 'gyur te/_/mi rtog rnam par rtog pa'i sa/_/stong pa nyid ni yang dag mthong /_/sgyu ma 
[\[163b.6\]]glang po sna tshogs ltar/_/lo ma rnams dang gser bzhin du/_/mi shes pas ni sems bsgos pas/_/de bzhin mi la snang ba mngon/_/'phags pas nor ba mi mthong ste/_/yang dag nyid kyang skabs der min/_/gang phyir skabs der yang dag na/_/nor pa yang dag nyid du 'gyur/_/
[\[163b.7\]]nor ba thams cad spangs nas kyang /_/gal te mtshan ma skyer zin na/_/de nyid de yi nor ba ste/_/rab rib yongs su ma dag bzhin/_/rab rib can rnams bslad pa yis/_/skra shad mthong ba ji bzhin du/_/de bzhin byis pa rnams kyi yang /_/'dzin pa yul la shin tu 'jug_/
[\[164a\]]
[\[164a.1\]]srid gsum 'di ni skra shad 'dra/_/smig rgyu chu ltar nor ba ste/_/rmi lam sgyu ma lta bur ni/_/rnam par bsgoms na grol bar 'gyur/_/rnam par rtog dang rnam brtag dang /_/rnam par rtog pa 'jug pa dang /_/'ching dang bcing bya bcings pa dang /_/drug po 'di dag thar 
[\[164a.2\]]pa'i rgyu/_/sa rnams med cing bden pa med/_/zhing rnams med cing sprul pa med/_/sangs rgyas rnams dang rang sangs rgyas/_/nyan thos rnams kyang brtags pa'o/_/gang zag rgyu dang phung po'i rnams/_/de bzhin rkyen dang rdul phran dang /_/gtso bo dbang phyug byed po rnams/_/
[\[164a.3\]]sems tsam la ni rnam par rtog_/sems ni thams cad thams cad du/_/thams cad lus la 'jug pa ste/_/rnam pa drug gis sna tshogs 'dzin/_/sems kyi rigs ni mtshan nyid med/_/phung po rnams la bdag med de/_/bdag kyang phung po rnams la med/_/de dag brtags 
[\[164a.4\]]pa bzhin du med/_/de dag med pa'ang ma yin no/_/ji ltar byis pas brtags pa bzhin/_/dngos po thams cad yod pa ste/_/de dag mthong bzhin yod na ni/_/thams cad yang dag mthong bar 'gyur/_/dngos po thams cad med pa'i phyir/_/kun nas nyon mongs dag 
[\[164a.5\]]pa med/_/ji ltar mthong bzhin de med de/_/de dag med pa'ang ma yin no/_/kun rtog nor ba'i mtshan ma ste/_/gzhan gyi dbang gi mtshan nyid do/_/mtshan nyid de la ming btags pa/_/de ni rnam brtags mtshan nyid do/_/rkyen dang ming gi brda dag las/_/kun rtog nor 
[\[164a.6\]]ba'i mtshan ma dag_/nam zhig de la mi 'byung na/_/yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/rnam par smin pa'i sangs rgyas dang /_/sprul pa yi ni rgyal ba dang /_/byang chub sems dpa' sems can dang /_/phyogs phyogs kyi ni zhing rnams dang /_/sgyu 'dra'i chos dang 'phrul pa dang /_/
[\[164a.7\]]rgyal ba gang rnams sprul pa dag_/de kun snang ba mtha' yas kyi/_/bde ba can nas byung ba'o/_/gang yang sprul pas bshad pa dang /_/rnam smin skyes pas gang bshad pa/_/mdo sde shin tu rgyas pa'i tshul/_/de yi dgongs pa shes par gyis/_/gang yang rgyal ba'i bus bshad 
[\[164b\]]
[\[164b.1\]]dang /_/gang yang 'dren pa rnams kyis bshad/_/sprul pa dag gis gang bshad pa/_/rnam smin rgyal bas ma yin no/_/chos 'di rnams ni ma skyes pa/_/de dag pa yang ma yin te/_/dri za'i grong khyer sgyu ma dang /_/rmi lam sprul pa rnams dang 'dra/_/sna tshogs sems ni 'jug pa ste/_/
[\[164b.2\]]sems ni rnam par grol bar 'gyur/_/'gog par 'gyur ba'ang sems nyid de/_/sems nyid skye yi gzhan ma yin/_/mi yi sna tshogs brtags pa'i sems/_/don du snang ba mngon pa ste/_/'di ni sems tsam don kyang med/_/rnam par mi rtog grol bar 'gyur/_/thog ma med 
[\[164b.3\]]dus spros pa yis/_/gnas ngan len rnams rab tu bsdus/_/rnam par rtog pa bsgos pas na/_/de phyir log par snang ba 'byung /_/don du snang la rnam shes te/_/ye shes yang dag spyod yul can/_/shin tu gyur pa snang med de/_/de ni 'phags pa'i spyod yul lo/_/don rab 
[\[164b.4\]]'byed pa'i bsam gtan dang /_/byis pa nyer spyod bsam gtan dang /_/de bzhin nyid dmigs bsam gtan pas/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsam gtan dge/_/kun brtags pa yi rang bzhin gyis/_/thams cad chos ni ma skyes pa/_/gzhan gyi dbang la gnas nas ni/_/

[\[165a.1\]]yod dang med pa gang rtog par/_/mu stegs lta ba can dag ni/_/bstan las ring du gyur par shes/_/dngos po kun gyi smra ba la/_/rtag tu tshe rabs gzhan song tshe/_/phan tshun rnam par rtog pa yis/_/goms shing goms par byed pa'o/_/ming ni brjod par

[\[165a.3\]]rtog/_/'gags pa med cing ma skyes te/_/rang bzhin gyis ni nam mkha' 'dra/_/dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin gang /_/de dag rnam brtags mtshan nyid do/_/mig yor 'dra zhing sgyu ma bzhin/_/smig rgyu rmi lam mgal me bskor/_/dri za'i grong khyer brag ca 'dra/_/chos de rkyen

[\[165b.1\]]bzhin nyid/_/yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/pha ma phrad par gyur pa'i phyir/_/kun gzhi yid dang ldan pa ni/_/mar gyi bum pa'i byi ba bzhin/_/khu chu dang ni lhan cig skyes/_/sla gar rman bu 'bras mi sdug_/las kyis rnam pa sna tshogs byas/_/

[\[166a.1\]]mthong /_/mtshan ma sna tshogs de dag ni/_/mu stegs can lam khrid par byed/_/nyan thos dag dang rang rgyal gyi/_/spyod yul du yang ltung bar byed/_/yul la 'dzin pa med pa'i phyir/_/'gog pa med pa ma yin te/_/'phags pa rnams kyi spyod yul ltar/_/de bzhin nyid kyi dngos

[\[169a.1\]]chos rnams thams cad rtogs pa'i phyir/_/sangs rgyas ye shes rnam dag 'gyur/_/mig dang gzugs dang snang ba dang /_/yid dang nam mkha' de bzhin te/_/de dag gis ni mi rnams kyi/_/kun gzhi las byung rnam shes te/_/gzung dang 'dzin dang 'dzin byed pa/_/

[\[169a.4\]]yod dang med las 'da' bar bya/_/log pa'i 'du shes bdag med par/_/mthong bas srid la 'dun mi bya/_/brtag pa byed dang bcas pa ni/_/rtsod pa tsam zhig 'jug pa ste/_/bden pa'i mchog ni smrar med pa/_/'gags na chos ni mthong ba yin/_/kun gzhi la ni rten bcas nas/_/yid 
[\[169a.5\]]kyang shin tu 'jug par 'gyur/_/sems dang yid la rten bcas nas/_/rnam par shes pa 'jug par 'gyur/_/sgro btags pa dang sgro 'dogs pa/_/sems kyi chos nyid de bzhin nyid/_/rnal 'byor pas ni de bsgoms na/_/sems tsam shes pa thob par 'gyur/_/yid dang mtshan 
[\[169a.6\]]nyid dngos po la/_/rtag dang mi rtag rlom sems med/_/skye dang mi skyer rnal 'byor la/_/rnal 'byor pa ni rlom sems med/_/kun gzhi las byung rnam shes ni/_/don gnyis la ni rtog par byed/_/de las byung ba'i don gcig la/_/sems gnyis kyis ni mi shes 'gyur/_/smra ba med 
[\[169a.7\]]cing tshig med de/_/sems mthong phyir ni stong ma yin/_/rang gi sems ni ma mthong phyir/_/lta ba'i dra ba 'jug par 'gyur/_/rkyen rnams 'ong ba med pa ste/_/dngos po dag ni gang yang med/_/khams rnams med cing phung po med/_/'dod chags med cing 'dus byas med/_/las dang 
[\[169b\]]
[\[169b.1\]]me med sngon yang med/_/byas pa med cing 'dus byas med/_/mtha' yang med cing chags pa med/_/thar pa med cing bcings pa med/_/lung ma bstan la dngos po med/_/chos dang chos ma yin pa med/_/dus med mya ngan 'das pa med/_/chos nyid dag kyang med pa yin/_/sangs rgyas med cing bden rnams med/_/'bras 
[\[169b.2\]]bu med cing rgyu rnams med/_/log dang mya ngan 'das pa med/_/'byung ba dang ni 'jig pa med/_/yan lag bcu gnyis yod ma yin/_/mtha' dang mtha' med pa yang med/_/lta ba thams cad spang ba'i phyir/_/sems tsam du ni ngas bshad do/_/lus dang nyon mongs las lam dang /_/
[\[169b.3\]]byed pa po dang 'bras bu rnams/_/rmi lam dang ni smig rgyu 'dra/_/dri za yi ni grong khyer bzhin/_/sems tsam du ni rnam bzhag pas/_/dngos po'i mtshan las rnam par ldog_/sems tsam la ni gnas pa'i phyir/_/rtag dang chad pa mthong bar 'gyur/_/mya ngan 'das la phung po med/_/
[\[169b.4\]]bdag kyang med cing mtshan nyid med/_/sems tsam la ni 'jug pa yis/_/thar pa 'dzin las log par 'gyur/_/sa snang rgyus byung nges pa ni/_/mi rnams la ni phyi rol snang /_/sems ni snang ba med las byung /_/de yi phyir na sems mi snang /_/mi rnams kyis ni bag chags rnams/_/lus 
[\[169b.5\]]dang longs spyod gnas ltar snang /_/sems ni dngos min dngos med min/_/bag chags dag gis mi mdzes so/_/dkar po la ni dri ma snang /_/dri ma la ni dkar mi snang /_/nam mkha' la ni sprin bzhin du/_/de bzhin sems kyang mi snang ngo /_/sems ni las kyang sogs par byed/_/ye shes kyis ni 
[\[169b.6\]]rnam par sel/_/shes rab kyis ni snang med dang /_/mthu yang rab tu thob par byed/_/sems ni yul dang 'brel pa ste/_/shes pa rtog ge dag la 'jug_/snang ba med dang khyad par la/_/shes rab dag la shin tu 'jug_/sems dang yid dang rnam shes dang /_/'du shes rtog pa spangs pa ste/_/
[\[169b.7\]]rnam par mi rtog chos thob pas/_/nyan thos dag ni rgyal sras min/_/zhi ba bzod pa'i khyad par la/_/de bzhin gshegs kyi ye shes dge/_/kun tu spyod pa spangs pa ni/_/khyad par phyir ni skye bar 'gyur/_/kun tu brtags pa'i rang bzhin yod/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni med pa ste/_/nor pa dag 
[\[170a\]]
[\[170a.1\]]gis brtags pa 'dzin/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni mi rtag go/_/thams cad 'byung 'gyur gzugs med de/_/'byung las ma gyur gzugs yod do/_/dri za'i grong khyer rmi lam dang /_/sgyu ma smig rgyu 'byung 'gyur min/_/nga yi shes rab rnam gsum ste/_/gang gis 'phags pa rab phye ba/_/gang gis yang dag mtshan 
[\[170a.2\]]nyid rtog_/gang gis dngos po 'byed pa'o/_/nyan thos shes rab snang spangs pa/_/theg pa gnyis dang bral ba ni/_/dngos po yod par chags pas na/_/shin tu 'jug par 'gyur ba yin/_/sems tsam la ni 'jug pa yin/_/bde gshegs shes rab dri ma med/_/yod pa dang ni med pa las/_/rkyen gyis 
[\[170a.3\]]gal te skyer zin na/_/gcig dang tha dad lta ba la/_/gdon mi za bar de dag gnas/_/yan lag mang pos bsgrubs pa yi/_/sgyu ma bsgrub par mi 'gyur bar/_/de bzhin mtshan ma sna tshogs kyang /_/brtags na 'grub par mi 'gyur ro/_/mtshan ma gnas ngan len pa yi/_/'ching ba sems las byung ba ste/_/kun 
[\[170a.4\]]tu brtags pa mi shes pas/_/gzhan gyi dbang zhes rtog par 'gyur/_/gang brtags pa yi dngos po 'di/_/gzhan gyi dbang yang de nyid do/_/brtags pa sna tshogs snang ba yang /_/gzhan gyi dbang gis rtog par byed/_/kun rdzob dang ni dam pa'i don/_/rgyu las skyes pa gsum du med/_/brtags pa kun 
[\[170a.5\]]rdzob ces bya ste/_/de chad 'phags pa'i spyod yul lo/_/rnal 'byor can gyi dngos po ltar/_/sna tshogs gcig tu rnam par mdzes/_/de la rnam pa sna tshogs med/_/brtags pa'i mtshan kyang de bzhin no/_/ji ltar rab rib can dag gis/_/gzugs mthong ba la sna tshogs rtog_/rab rib gzugs min 
[\[170a.6\]]gzugs med min/_/mkhas pas gzhan dbang de bzhin no/_/ji ltar gser ni dag pa dang /_/chu yang rnyog pa spangs pa dang /_/nam mkha' la ni sprin med ltar/_/rnam rtog dag pa de bzhin no/_/nga yi nyan thos rnam gsum ste/_/sprul dang smon lam las skyes pa/_/'dod chags zhe sdang spangs pa yi/_/chos 
[\[170a.7\]]las byung ba'i nyan thos so/_/byang chub sems dpa' 'ang rnam gsum ste/_/sangs rgyas la ni mtshan nyid med/_/sems can dag gi sems sems la/_/sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab tu snang /_/brtags pa yi ni dngos po med/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni yod pa ste/_/sgro 'dogs pa dang skur pa ni/_/rnam par 
[\[170b\]]
[\[170b.1\]]brtags na 'jig par 'gyur/_/gal te brtags pa'i dngos med la/_/gzhan gyi dbang gi dngos yod na/_/dngos po med par dngos por 'gyur/_/dngos po dngos med las byung 'gyur/_/kun tu brtags la gnas bcas te/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni dmigs par 'gyur/_/ming dang mtshan ma 'brel pa las/_/

[\[170b.3\]]yin te/_/rang bzhin gsum yang de bzhin no/_/rnal 'byor can gyis de bsgoms na/_/de bzhin nyid las mi 'da'o/_/skar ma dang ni sprin lta bur/_/nyi ma zla ba 'dra ba dang /_/bag chags las byung snang 'dra ba/_/mi rnams kyi ni sems snang ngo /_/'byung ba 'di dag lus ma red/_/mtshan nyid med cing mtshan 
[\[170b.4\]]gzhi med/_/ji ste 'byung ba'i 'byung yin la/_/gzugs ni 'byung las gyur yin na/_/'byung ba chen po yang dag min/_/'byung ba 'byung las gyur pa med/_/'byung ba chen po rgyu yin te/_/'bras bu sa dang chu la sogs/_/rdzas dang btags dang gzugs rnams dang /_/sgyu ma'i rnam byas de bzhin te/_/rmi lam dri za'i 
[\[170b.5\]]grong gi gzugs/_/smig rgyu ba dang lnga yin no/_/'dod pa can ni rnam lnga dang /_/rigs kyang de bzhin lnga yin te/_/theg pa lnga dang theg pa med/_/mya ngan 'das pa rnam drug yin/_/phung po tha dad nyi shu bzhi/_/gzugs kyang rnam pa brgyad du 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas nyi shu rtsa bzhi yin/_/rgyal ba'i sras ni rnam 
[\[170b.6\]]pa gnyis/_/tshul yang brgya po rtsa brgyad de/_/de bzhin nyan thos rnam gsum mo/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi zhing gcig ste/_/de bzhin sangs rgyas gcig tu 'gyur/_/rnam par grol ba de bzhin gsum/_/sems kyi rgyud ni rnam pa bzhi/_/nga yi bdag med rnam pa drug_/shes par bya ba'ang rnam pa bzhi/_/rgyu rnams 
[\[170b.7\]]dang ni bral gyur pa/_/lta ba'i nyes pa rnam par spangs/_/so so rang rig mi g.yo ba/_/theg pa chen po bla na med/_/skye ba dang ni mi skye ba/_/rnam pa brgyad dang rnam dgur 'gyur/_/gcig dang rim gyis rtogs pa ste/_/grub pa'i mtha' yang gcig nyid do/_/gzugs med khams ni rnam pa brgyad/_/bsam 
[\[171a\]]
[\[171a.1\]]gtan tha dad rnam pa drug_/rang rgyal sras po rnams kyis ni/_/'byung ba rnam pa bdun du 'gyur/_/dus gsum po yang yod ma yin/_/rtag pa dang ni mi rtag med/_/byed dang las dang 'bras bu rnams/_/rmi lam gyi ni bya ba bzhin/_/sangs rgyas nyan thos rgyal ba'i sras/_/thog ma tha mar 'byung ba 
[\[171a.2\]]med/_/sems kyi snang dang bral ba ste/_/rtag tu sgyu ma'i chos dang 'dra/_/rum dang 'khor lo skye de bzhin/_/'byung dang dga' ldan gyi gnas dang /_/zhing kun tu ni gshegs par yang /_/snang yang mngal nas skye ba min/_/'pho dang rgyu dang sems can dang /_/de bzhin ston dang mya ngan 'das/_/bden pa 
[\[171a.3\]]dang ni zhing rtogs pa/_/rkyen rnams kyis ni 'phangs pa yin/_/'jig rten dang ni nags tshal gling /_/mu stegs 'pho dang bdag med dang /_/bsam gtan theg gnas thob pa dang /_/'bras bu'i spyod yul bsam mi khyab/_/zla ba skar ma'i rigs rnams dang /_/rgyal po'i rigs dang lha yi rnams/_/
[\[171a.4\]]gnod sbyin dri za'i rigs rnams ni/_/las las skyes shing srid las byung /_/bsam gyis mi khyab 'gyur ba na/_/shi 'pho bag chags ldan pa ste/_/shi 'phor snang ba chad na ni/_/nyon mongs dra ba 'gag par 'gyur/_/ba lang lug dang bran rnams dang /_/de bzhin glang po rta la sogs/_/nor dang 'bru 
[\[171a.5\]]dang gser rnams dang /_/zhing gi dngos por rnam par rtog_/khri dag la yang nyal mi bya/_/sa la yang ni mi bsku'o/_/gser dang dngul dang khar ba dang /_/zangs kyi lhung bzed mi bcang ngo /_/snam bu sngon por btsos pa dang /_/ba lang lci ba ngur smrig dang /_/sa dang 'bras bu lo ma yis/_/rnal 'byor 
[\[171a.6\]]rtag tu dkar po bsgyur/_/lcags dang rdo dang kham pa dang /_/dung dang shel las byas pa yi/_/ma ga d+hA yi bre gang tshad/_/lhung bzed rnal 'byor can gyis bcang /_/dgu po gos dra'i gri yang ni/_/sor bzhi pa yi tshad du bya/_/rnal 'byor gzhol ba'i rnal 'byor pas/_/bzo yi rig pa mi bslab bo/_/
[\[171a.7\]]rnal 'byor zhugs pa'i rnal 'byor pas/_/nyo dang btsong bar mi bya'o/_/kun dga'i khyim pas de byas te/_/de ni chos su ngas bshad do/_/dbang po bsdams shing don shes pas/_/mdo dang 'dul la de bzhin te/_/khyim pa dang ni ma 'dres par/_/rnal 'byor can la de ngas bshad/_/stong pa'i khyim 'am 
[\[171b\]]
[\[171b.1\]]dur khrod dam/_/shing gi drung dang phug rnams dang /_/bla gab med pa'am sog khrod du/_/rnal 'byor can gyis gnas bca'o/_/dur khrod du 'am gzhan gang du'ang /_/rtag par gos gsum bgo bar bya/_/gang yang bde bar byin pa dag_/gos kyi phyir ni sbyar bar bya/_/slongs mo'i zas la gzhol byas te/_/
[\[171b.2\]]gnya' shing gang du blta bar bya/_/me tog la ni bung ba ltar/_/de ltar bsod snyoms spyad par bya/_/mang po'i tshogs dang 'dres pa dang /_/dge slong ma dang gang 'dres pa/_/'dres par gyur pa'i 'tsho ba de/_/rnal 'byor can la rung ma yin/_/rnal 'byor gzhol ba'i rnal 'byor pas/_/rgyal po dang ni rgyal 
[\[171b.3\]]po'i bu/_/de bzhin blon po tshong dpon la/_/zas kyi phyir ni bsten mi bya/_/shing dang btsas pa'i khyim zas dang /_/dga' dang mdza' dang ldan pa dang /_/dge slong dge slong ma dang 'dres/_/rnal 'byor can la de mi rung /_/du ba gar 'thul gtsug lag khang /_/cho ga bzhin du rtag 'tshed dang /_/bdag gi 
[\[171b.4\]]phyir ni gang byas pa/_/rnal 'byor can la de mi rung /_/'jig rten mtshan gzhi mtshan ldan la/_/skye dang 'jig las grol ba dang /_/yod dang med kyi phyogs spangs pa/_/rnal 'byor can gyis rnal 'byor bsgoms/_/gal te skye bar mi rtog na/_/ting nge 'dzin dang stobs rnams dang /_/mngon par shes 
[\[171b.5\]]dang dbang ldan gyis/_/rnal 'byor par ni myur du 'gyur/_/rdul phran dus dang gtso bo dang /_/byed pa las kyang mi brtag go/_/'jig rten rgyu rkyen las byung bar/_/rnal 'byor pas ni mi brtag go/_/'jig rten rang gi rtog pas brtags/_/sna tshogs bag chags dag las byung /_/srid pa sgyu ma rmi 'dra bar/_/
[\[171b.6\]]rnal 'byor can gyis rtag tu ltos/_/sgro 'dogs pa dang skur pa yis/_/spangs par lta ba rtag tu ste/_/zhi ba'i lam ni dger song nas/_/rnal 'byor sna tshogs rtsom par byed/_/lus dang longs spyod gnas 'dra bar/_/srid pa gsum la ma rtogs shig_/zas kyi bya ba byas pa dang /_/bsam pa med par 
[\[171b.7\]]lus bsrang nas/_/sangs rgyas dang ni byang chub la/_/yang dang yang du phyag 'tshal te/_/rnal 'byor shes pas mdo 'dul dang /_/'thad pas yang dag bsdus nas ni/_/chos lnga dang ni rang sems dang /_/bdag med pa la rnam par sgoms/_/so so rang rig chos nyid gtsang /_/sa rnams dang ni sangs rgyas sa/_/rnal 
[\[172a\]]
[\[172a.1\]]'byor can gyis de bsgoms na/_/pad mo che la dbang bskur 'gyur/_/'gro ba kun tu rab 'khyams pas/_/srid pa la ni skyo ba'i yid/_/rnal 'byor can gyis brtson byas na/_/nyi ma zla ba'i dbyibs 'dra dang /_/'od dang pad mo lta bu dang /_/ri mo nam mkha'i me 'dra mthong /_/

[\[172a.3\]]de/_/yang dag rjes su 'gro ba'i ltas/_/rgyu med pa las dngos po ni/_/rtag dang chad pa spangs pa yi/_/yod med phyogs bral yod do zhes/_/dbu mar rnam par rtog par 'gyur/_/rgyu med par ni smra bas ni/_/rgyu med chad par lta bar rtog_/phyi rol dngos po ma rtogs phyir/_/dbu ma 'jig par byed

[\[172a.5\]]yin te snang ba med/_/snang ba med pas mi skye ste/_/dbu ma yi ni lam 'di yang /_/nga dang gzhan dag gis kyang bshad/_/skye ba dang ni mi skye dang /_/dngos dang dngos med stong pa dang /_/dngos po rang bzhin med pa ni/_/gnyis po de dag rnam mi brtag_/rnam par rtog pa 'jug med pas/_/byis pa rnams ni thar par 
[\[172a.6\]]rtog_/sems 'jug pa ni ma rtogs pas/_/'dzin pa gnyis ni spong mi 'gyur/_/rang gi sems snang rtogs pa'i phyir/_/'dzin pa rnam gnyis spong bar 'gyur/_/spong dang yongs su 'dzin pa ni/_/rnam rtog 'jig par byed ma yin/_/sems snang yongs su shes pa'i phyir/_/rnam par rtog pa mi 'jug go/_/

[\[172b.1\]]mu stegs can gyi smra bar 'gyur/_/mi skye ba la skye ba mang /_/shi 'phos med cing 'chi 'phor 'gyur/_/zhing rnams bye bar cig car yang /_/chu yi zla ba bzhin du snang /_/gcig dang mang por gyur nas ni/_/me yang 'bar zhing char yang 'bebs/_/sems la sems kyir gyur nas ni/_/de dag sems tsam du yang 
[\[172b.2\]]smra/_/sems rnams la ni sems tsam dang /_/sems med par ni sems las byung /_/rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs dang dbyibs/_/sems tsam du ni rtogs par rig_/thub dbang dang ni nyan thos gzugs/_/rang rgyal dang yang 'dra ba dang /_/rnam pa mang po'i gzugs gzhan gyis/_/sems tsam du ni de

[\[172b.4\]]rang gi rnam rtog phyin ci log_/spros pa dang ni g.yo ba dang /_/thos mthong shes dang rtogs pa dang /_/'du shes dag gis bus pa bcings/_/gzhan gyi dbang ni mtshan ma ste/_/gang ming de ni kun brtags pas/_/kun rtog pa dang mtshan ma ni/_/gzhan gyi dbang las shin tu 'byung /_/blo yis rab tu gzhig 
[\[172b.5\]]nas ni/_/brtag pa med cing dbang yang med/_/grub pa'i dngos po yod med na/_/ji ltar blo yis rnam par brtag_/yod dang med pa spangs pa yi/_/grub pa yi ni dngos po yod/_/dngos po yod med rnam grol ba'i/_/rang bzhin gnyis su ji ltar 'gyur/_/
[\[172b.6\]]kun tu brtags pa'i rang bzhin la/_/rang bzhin dag ni gnyis gnas te/_/brtags pa rnam pa sna tshogs snang /_/rnam dag 'phags pa'i spyod yul lo/_/brtags pa sna tshogs snang ba la/_/gzhan gyi dbang zhes rtog byed de/_/rnam pa gzhan du

[\[173a\]]
[\[173a.1\]]'phags pa lang kar gshegs pa/_bam po dgu pa ste tha ma/_zhing dang sangs rgyas sprul pa dang /_/theg pa gcig dang de bzhin gsum/_/mya ngan 'das dang nga med de/_/thams cad stong pa skye spangs pa'o/_/sangs rgyas bye brag sum cu drug_/bye brag dag ni bcu po dang /_/
[\[173a.2\]]sems can so so'i sems rgyud rnams/_/de dag zhing gi snod yin no/_/ji ltar brtags pa'i dngos po ni/_/sna tshogs blta bar snang mod kyi/_/sna tshogs nyid ni de la med/_/'gro ba de bzhin chos sangs rgyas/_/chos kyi sangs rgyas sangs rgyas yin/_/lhag ma de yi sprul pa'o/_/sems can 
[\[173a.3\]]rang gi sa bon rgyud/_/sangs rgyas snang bar mthong ba'o/_/mtshan ma 'brel las nor ba yi/_/rnam par rtog pa rab tu 'jug_/_rtog las de bzhin nyid gzhan min/_/mtshan ma las kyang rnam rtog min/_/rang bzhin gyi dang rdzogs longs spyod/_/'phrul pa dang ni sprul pa gang /_/sangs rgyas 
[\[173a.4\]]mang po sum cu drug_/rang bzhin gyi ni sangs rgyas yin/_/sngon po dmar po lan tshwa dang /_/dung dang 'o ma kha ra dang /_/bska ba'i 'bras bu me tog sogs/_/ji ltar nyi las 'od zer dang /_/rgya mtsho rlabs dang ldan pa ni/_/mi gcig tha dad ma yin te/_/sems dang lhan cig ldan pa yi/_/rnam 
[\[173a.5\]]shes bdun po de bzhin no/_/rgya mtsho yongs su gyur pa ni/_/dba' rlabs sna tshogs de dag ste/_/rnam par shes zhes bya ba ste/_/kun gzhi de bzhin sna tshogs 'byung /_/sems dang yid dang rnam shes pa/_/mtshan nyid don du rab tu brtag_/brgyad po tha dad ma yin mtshan/_/mtshan gyi gzhi med 
[\[173a.6\]]mtshan nyid med/_/rgya mtsho dang ni dba' rlabs rnams/_/ji ltar khyad par med pa ltar/_/de bzhin sems la rnam shes dag_/yongs su gyur pa mi rnyed do/_/sems kyis las rnams sogs par byed/_/yid kyis kyang ni rnam par sogs/_/rnam par shes pas rnam par rig_/lnga pa 
[\[173a.7\]]snang la rtag par byed/_/sngon po dmar po'i rnam par ni/_/mi yi rnam par shes par gda'/_/dba' rlabs sems dang chos 'thun pa/_/ji srid thub chen bshad du gsol/_/sngon po dmar po'i rnam pa rnams/_/dba' rlabs dag la ma mchis la/_/mtshan nyid don du byis pa la/_/
[\[173b\]]
[\[173b.1\]]sems kyi 'jug pa dag kyang bshad/_/de la 'jug pa med pa ste/_/rang gi sems ni gzung ba spangs/_/gzung ba yod na 'dzin pa yang /_/dba' rlabs 'thun par bsgrub par 'gyur/_/lus dang longs spyod gnas lta bur/_/mi yi rnam par shes pa snang /_/de phyir de yi 'jug pa ni/_/
[\[173b.2\]]dba' rlabs dag dang 'dra bar snang /_/dba' rlabs dngos por rgya mtsho ni/_/gar byed rnam par rig 'gyur na/_/kun gzhi 'jug pa de bzhin du/_/ci yi phyir ni blos mi rig_/byis pa blo dang mi ldan pas/_/kun gzhi rgya mtsho ji bzhin du/_/dba' rlabs 'byung ba'i chos 'thun phyir/_/
[\[173b.3\]]dpe yis nye bar sbyar ba yin/_/bzang ngan bar ma'i skye bo la/_/gdugs 'char 'gyur ba ji bzhin du/_/de bzhin 'jig rten sgron ma khyod/_/byis pa rnams la yang dag ston/_/chos la rab tu zhugs mdzad cing /_/ji slad de bzhin nyid mi gsung /_/de ste de bzhin nyid gsung na/_/

[\[174a.1\]]ma yin te/_/ji ltar nad pa nad pa la/_/sman pas sman rnams gtong ba ltar/_/sangs rgyas de bzhin sems can la/_/sems tsam du yang rab tu gsung /_/phyi rol bag chags sa bon gyis/_/rnam par rtog pa rab tu 'byung /_/gang gis 'dzin pa dbang yin te/_/gang bzung de
